itaṉ etiroli pōlak kōṭṭi muḻumiyumē, uyir vantatu pōl, ‘rōm vāḻka! kōriyōlāṉas vāḻka!’ eṉṟu āravārittirintatu.
vālṣiyar paṭivīṭu maṭṭum oḷiyiḻantatu.
kōriyōlāṉas vīramum avaṉ uyirum avaṉ uṭali viṭṭu viṭi koṇṭu ceṉṟuviṭṭaṉa eṉṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu.
ciṅkam pōṉṟa iṟumāppuṭiya avaṉ uruvam ciṟiyatoru muyaleṉa oṭuṅki naṭuṅkiṟṟu.
avaṉ taṉ vāḷi uṟiyiliṭṭu viṭṭu nērāka āḵpītiyas irukkiyi aṇuki, avaṉiṭam avvāḷi uṟiyuṭaṉ koṭuttut ‘tāy nāṭṭiṉ pakivaṉ, taṉ uṟaviṉar nāṭṭiṉ tuṇivaṉ āvaṉō? nāṉ uṅkaḷukkum pakivaṉē! eṉ uyirik koḷka’ eṉṟāṉ.
avaṉ vīraṉeṉṟu terintatum avaṉ mītuḷḷa poṟāmi oru puṟam; avaṉāl aṭinta ēmāṟṟam orupuṟam āka niṉṟu āḵpītiyasiyum avaṉ vīrarākiya vālṣiyariyum maṉita uṇarcciyaṟṟavarākac ceytu viṭṭatu.
paṭiyiḻantu pakiyuṇarcciyum iḻanta avaṉi avarkaḷ avaṉ vāymoḻikkiṇaṅkavē, ‘tāynāṭṭukkup pakivaṉ, aṇṭiyavarkkum pakivaṉ, maṉita vakuppukkē oru kōṭārik kāmpu’ eṉak kūvikkoṇṭu avaṉic cūḻntu vāḷālum īṭṭiyālum kaṭṭiyālum tākki veṭṭiyum kuttiyum eṟintum aṭittum avaṉik koṉṟu avaṉuṭalic citittaṉar.
piṉ avarkaḷ avaṉ kuṭali īṭṭiyil kuttik koṭiyākap piṭittukkoṇṭu, rōm mītu taṅkaḷukkuḷḷa cīṟṟamaṉittiyum avaṉ mītē kāṭṭi, rōm makkaḷ kaṇkāṇa rōm nakark kōṭṭiyic cuṟṟic ceṉṟu, ‘kōriyōlāṉas vīḻka! vālṣiyar pakivaṉ vīḻka; vañcaka rōmaṉ vīḻka!’ eṉṟu kataṟiṉar.
avaṉ muṭivaṟinta rōmar, tī mitittavar pōlat tuṭittār. varjīliyā attuṇukkuṟu ceyti kātil viḻāmuṉ orē alaṟāy alaṟi vīḻntu māṇṭāḷ. 
valamṉiyāvum, taṉ makaṉi - taṉ vīra makaṉi - naṉṟikeṭṭa annakarukkē palikoṭutta tuyar tāḷātu vāḷāl taṉṉi māyttuk koṇṭāḷ.
vāḻvu nāḷil pakittuk koṇṭē kōriyōlāṉasi iḻantu tāḻvaṭintu, nāṇiḻanta naṅkipōl nalaṉiḻantu māḻkiyatu rōm nakaram.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Caiws Marciws
2.	Vulumnia
3.	Tarquin 
4.	Menenius Agrippa
5.	Volsciaus	
6.	Tullus Aufidus 
7.	Consuls 
8.	Cominus 
9.	Titus Lartius
10.	Junius Brutus
11.	Sicinius Velutus
12.	Virgilia

3. antōṇiyum kiḷayōppātrāvum
Antonio and Cleopatra
kati uṟuppiṉar
āṭavar:
1. antōṇiyō: rōma vīraṉ, oppaṟṟa paṭittalivaṉ. āciya nāṭukaḷiyum ekiptiyum veṉṟaṭakkiyavaṉ - kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ ārūyirk kātalaṉ. avaḷ kātalāl vīra vāḻvum, araciyal vāḻvum iḻantavaṉ. mūvaruḷ mutalvaṉ.
2. akṭēviyas sīsar: mūvaruḷ oruvaṉ-jūliyas sīsariṉ putalvaṉ-araciyal cūḻcciyil vallavaṉ. maṟṟa iruvariyum etirikaḷiyum vittuc cokkaṭṭāṉaṭiya talivaṉ.
3. leppiṭas: mūvaruḷ kaṭiyāṉavaṉ-akṭēviyasāl antōṇiyiṉ āṟṟalik kuṟikka uyarttappaṭṭa peruṅkuṭi makaṉ.  celvaṉ, veḷḷiyuḷḷamum, peruntaṉmiyu muṭiyavaṉ.
4. pāmpi: mūvariṉ potu etiri. kaṭaṟpaṭi valimi yuṭiyavaṉ. peruntaṉmiyum, nampikkiyum uṭiyavaṉ.
5. eṉōpārpas: kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ amiccaṉ; paṭit talivaṉ; araṇmaṉip paṇiyāḷar talivaṉ. naṇpaṉ. kātal tōḻaṉ.
6. antōṇiyiṉ tuṇittalivaṉ: antōṇiyiṭam paṟṟuṭiyavaṉāyiṉum tavaṟukaḷik kaṇṭikka etiriyiṭam ceṉṟu avaṉ perun taṉmiyiṉāl piṉṉum kaḻivirakkaṅ koṇṭavaṉ.
7. tūtar:
peṇṭir:
1. kiḷiyōppātrā: ekiptu araci. ṭālamikaḷiṉ vaḻi vantavaḷ. aḻaki, oppaṟṟa col tiṟamuṭiyavaḷ. sīsar, pāmpi mutaliyavarik kātal tiṟamiyākak koṇṭu antōṇiyiṉ kātaliṟkut taṉṉit tiṟiyākkik koṭuttavaḷ.
2. palviyā: antōṇiyiṉ mutal maṉivi.
3. akṭēviyā: antōṇiyiṉ iraṇṭām maṉivi.
katic curukkam
jūliyas sīsarukkup piṉ rōm araciyal mūvar kiyil cikkiyatu. potumakkaḷ uḷḷaṅ kavarnta vīraṉ antōṇi, jūliyas sīsar makaṉ akṭēviyal sīsar, leppiṭas eṉṟa celvap peruṅkuṭi makaṉ ākiyavarē immūvar, avaruḷ antōṇiyē oppaṟṟa vīraṉātaliṉ kīḻ nāṭukaḷaṉittiyum ekiptiyum veṉṟaṭakkiṉāṉ. āṉāl muṉ sīsariyum pāmpiyiyuṅ kātal cūḻalil tiṟikoṇṭa araci kiḷiyōppātrā avaṉiyum kavarntāḷ.
araciyal cūḻcci valla akṭēviyasiṉic cilanāḷ antōṇiyiṉ mutal maṉivi palviyā etirttu niṉṟāḷ. avaḷ iṟantapiṉ akṭēviyas antōṇiyi varuvittut taṉ oṉṟuviṭṭa taṅki akṭōviyāvi avaṉukku maṇantu avaṉip piṇittāṉ. piṉ avaṉutaviyāl pāmpi eṉpavaṉit tammuṭaṉ naṭpāyc ceytāṉ.
āṉāl virivil pāmpi iṟantāṉ. leppiṭasi nayamākak kuṟṟañcāṭṭi akṭēviyas koṉṟāṉ. piṉ antōṇiyum paki toṭukka muyaṉṟāṉ. kiḷiyōppātrā kātal vāḻvāl, avaṉ ellā nāṭum piṭikkumaḷavum antōṇi aciyavilli. ekipti etirtta piṉṉum avaḷ piṇakku vaḻi niṉṟu pōril tōlvikaḷ peṟṟāṉ. iṟutiyil tam kātal vāḻvilēyē aḻunti iruvarum taṟkoli ceytu ekipti akṭēviyasukku viṭuttaṉar. ulakiyal cūḻcciyil valla akṭēviyaskūṭa antōṇiyiṉ vīrattiṉ arumiyiyum ataṉi viḻuṅkiya kātaliṉ perumiyiyum pārāṭṭiṉāṉ.
1. veṉṟi vīraṉiyum veṉṟa melliyalāḷ
kiḷiyōppātrā ekiptiṉ oppaṟṟa araci. avaḷ kariya niṟamuṭiya vaḷāyiṉum mācu maṟuvaṟṟa vaṭivaḻaki. avaḷ taṉ oru pārviyāl ulakiyē āṭṭi vikkum tiṟamuṭiyavaḷ.
makkaḷi aṟiyum nuṇtiṟattilum, avarkaḷi makiḻcciyuḷ āḻttum nayattilum  avaḷukku yārum īṭu aṟṟavar. avaḷ nāttiṟaṉō collun taramuṭiyataṉṟu. ulakiṉ kaviñarkaḷum nāṭaka āciriyarkaḷum avaḷiṭamiruntē tam kalittiṟaṉik kaṭaṉ vāṅkavēṇṭum.
ekiptu nāṭṭu makkaḷ māyattiṟkum mantirattiṟkum pēr pōṉavarkaḷ. avarkaḷkūṭa, avaḷ nāvacittāl ulakaciyum eṉṟum, avaḷ muka aḻaku caṟṟuk kuṟintiruntāl ulakiṉ varalāṟē muṟṟilum māṟiyirukku meṉṟum kūṟuvārām.
iṅṅaṉam kūṟiyatil viyappu etuvum illi. avaḷ mutalil maṇanta ekiptu aracaṉ ṭālmikkup piṉ, ulaki veṉṟa vīrarkaḷum rōmap peruntalivarkaḷumāṉa pāmpiyum sīsarum, oruvar piṉ oruvarāka avaḷ kavarcciyuṭpaṭṭu avaḷ kālaṭiyil tuvaṇṭukiṭantaṉar. avarkaḷ ulakiṉar añcum vīramum varalāṟṟil niṉṟu nilavum pukaḻum uṭiyavarāyiṉum, avaḷ aḻakiṉ muṉ, avaḷ māya miraṭṭalkaḷiṉ muṉ, talivaṇaṅki niṉṟaṉarēyaṉṟi vēṟāṉārallar.
sīsarukkup piṉ rōmap pēraracit taṉ vīrattālum tōḷ valiyālum āḷun tiṟamuṭiyavaṉ antōṇi eṉṟa vīraṉē. āyiṉum avaṉ peruntaṉmiyuṭiyavaṉ; iṉpa vāḻvilum, naṇparuṭaṉ kūṭik kulāvuvatilum viruppuṭiyavaṉ; araciyal perumiyi nāṭiyavaṉ allaṉ. ākavē rōmiṉ araciyal avaṉ kālaṭiyil kiṭappiṉum avaṉ ataṉi ēṟṟup pēraracaṉ ākāmal ataṉik kippantu pōl vittu viḷiyāṭiṉāṉ.
sīsariṉ makaṉākiya akṭēviyal sīsar antōṇiyiṉ maṉap pōkkiyum nāṭṭiṉ niliyiyum kaṇṭu, avaṟṟit taṉatu muṟpōkkiṟkuk karuviyākap payaṉpaṭutta eṇṇiṉāṉ. rōmap perumakkaḷuḷ celvākkuṭiya leppiṭas perumakaṉiyum ivveṇṇattiṟkēṟpa, avaṉ taṉṉuṭaṉ cērttuk koṇṭāṉ. immūvarum rōmap pēraraciṉ mītu oruṅku āṭci celuttuvatāka uṭaṉpaṭikki ceytukoṇṭaṉar. rōmap pēraraciṉ peyarāl kīḻnāṭukaḷi veṉṟaṭakkuvatāka antōṇi ēṟṟuk koṇṭāṉ. ataṉpaṭi avaṉ ceṉṟu pārttiyarum, pāktiyarum, pārasīkarum, ayōṉiyarum veruṇṭōṭumpaṭi pōr purintu piṉ ekiptiyum veṉṟu kīḻ aṭakkiṉāṉ. āyiṉ, antō! nañcūṭṭiya amiḻtākiya kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ vaṉappil īṭupaṭṭu avaṉ taṉ vīramum uyarvum iḻantatōṭu, taṉ veṟṟi viḻāvi aṟavē maṟantu kātal viḻā ayarvāṉāyiṉaṉ.
antōṇikkup palviyā eṉṟa oru maṉiviyuṇṭu. avaḷ mikunta aṟivum tiṟamiyum uṭiyavaḷ āyiṉum, uṇarcci vacappaṭṭa antōṇi pōṉṟa āṭavaṉik kaṭṭuppaṭuttap pōtiya kavarcciyaṟṟavaḷ. avaṉ uyarviyum vīrattiyum avaḷ naṉkaṟivāḷ. avaṉiyotta vīraṉik kaṇavaṉākap peṟṟataṟkāka avaḷ iṟampūtu eytiṉāḷ eṉpatil aiyamilli. āṉāl, avaṉ uḷḷattik koḷḷi koḷḷa avaḷāl muṭiyavilli. uyarkuṭiyiṟ piṟanta avaḷukku araciyal cūḻccikaḷ nalla pāṭamāyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟi etirkkum eṇṇam kaṇavaṉiṭam illi eṉpati avaḷ kaṇṭu avaṉ mītu cīṟṟam koṇṭāḷ. itaṉāl kātaluṟavu paṟantatu. avaṉ ceyya vēṇṭum araciyal naṭavaṭikkikaḷi eṭuttu avaḷ avaṉ araciyal amiccaṉākac ceyalāṟṟa maṭṭumē avaḷāl muṭintatu.
inniliyil avaḷ antōṇiyiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāṉ utaviyāl oru ciṟu paṭi cērttu sīsari etirttup pōriṭṭāḷ. iṅṅaṉam ceytatu uṇmiyil sīsariṉ vaṉmiyik kuṟippataṟkāka maṭṭumaṉṟu; antōṇiyi rōmiṟkuk koṇṭu varuvataṟkākavumēyām.
kēvalam, peṇṇākiya palviyā iṅṅaṉam āṭavarkaḷi vīrattilum tiṟaṉilum maṭakkuvatu teyvattiṟkup poṟukka villi pōlum! aḵtu inta ikkaṭṭāṉa niliyil avaḷit taṉ pakkam aḻittuk koṇṭatu.
kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ kaṇpārvi eṉṉum nilavoḷiyil avaḷatu karuṅkuḻaleṉṉum mukiliṉiṭiyē viḷiyāṭi, ulaka āṭciyiyum rōm araciyaliyum maṟantirunta antōṇi, palviyā iṟantatu kēṭṭu varuntuvataṟku māṟākat taṉ iṉpa vāḻviṉ iṭiyūṟu oṉṟu akaṉṟateṉṟu karuti makiḻntāṉ.
kiḷiyōppātrā ituvari vīrar, aracar-pēraracar mutaliya palar neñcam tiṟikoṇṭu avarkaṭku āṭpaṭātu niṉṟavaḷ eṉiṉum, antōṇiyik kaṇṭatumē avaṉpāl taṉ neñcam taṉṉiyum mīṟic celvati uṇarntāḷ. ituvariyiṟ piṟar neñcil vīṟuṟa irukki koṇṭataṉṟit taṉ neñcil piṟarkkiṭantarāta at tiyalar māṇikkam iṉṟu atē neñcil vēṟetaṟkumiṭamiṉṟi avaṉ vaṭivaḻakum paṇpaḻakum vantu niṟintati uṇarntāḷ.
ekiptil antōṇi vāḻum vāḻkkiyip paṟṟi rōmiṟ palarum palavāṟu tattam maṉam pōṉapaṭi pitaṟṟuvārāyiṉar, avaṟṟik kēṭṭu mūvar oppantattiṟ kiyeḻuttiṭṭa maṟṟa iruvarum maṉaṅ kavaṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷukkup potu etiriyākiya pāmpiyiṭam ariya kaṭaṟpaṭi mūṉṟu iruntatu. ataṉāl avarkaḷ eḷitiṟ pēraracu muṟṟum ceṉṟu aṭakkiyāḷa muṭiyātapaṭi avaṉ avarkaḷit taṭiceytu koṇṭiruntāṉ. antōṇiyiṉ muḻu ottuḻippiṉṟi avarkaḷāl avaṉi etirttaṭakka muṭiyāteṉṟu aṟintu, avarkaḷ antōṇikku mēṉmēlum tūtari aṉuppi araciyal vāḻvilīṭupaṭumāṟum, rōm nakar vantu tammuṭaṉ kalakkumāṟum vēṇṭiṉar.
āṉāl, ivvaraciyal tūtarkaḷ varavu antōṇiyiṉ kātal vāḻvākiya naṟuntēṉiṭiyē attēṉik kuṭikka voṭṭāmal keṭukka varum īkkaḷiṉ varavu pōṉṟiruntatu. ariya tuyiliḷāḻntavaṉ uṭaliṟ kocukkaṭittāl, avaṉ tūkkattōṭu tūkkamāka ataṉi aṟintu koṇṟu mīṇṭun tūṅkuvatupōla, avaṉ mutaṉmutal vanta tūtaṉi varavēṟkavō, avaṉ yāriṭamiruntu vantāṉeṉṟu kēṭkavō kūṭac ceyyāmal, paṇiyāṭkaḷ mūlamē avaṉit turattiviṭṭāṉ. itu kēṭṭu sīsar mikavum vekuṇṭā ṉeṉiṉum iṭamum vēḷiyumaṟintu ataṉi aṭakkik koṇṭu poṟumiyuṭaṉ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum āḷaṉuppik koṇṭēyiruntāṉ.
2. maṇamum araciyaluṟavukaḷum
‘aṭimēl aṭi aṭittāl ammiyum nakarum’ eṉṟa uṇmippaṭiyē, palviyāviṉ pirivuc ceytiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa nalluṇarcciyālō, antōṇiyiṉ maṉattiluṅ kūṭac cila nāḷil oruvakiyāṉa āṇmiyuṇarcciyum maṉaccāṉṟum eḻuntu avaṉatu vāḻkkip pōkki-cōmpal vāḻvik-kuṟikūṟat toṭaṅkiṉa. pakic ciṅkattiṉ uṟumal kēṭṭup peṇ ciṅkattiṉ kuḻiviyum kuṭṭikaḷiṉ naṭukkattiyum pārāmal vīṟiṭṭeḻum ciṅkavēṟṟip pōla avaṉ kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ kātalākiya paṭṭunūl valiyi aṟutteṟintu rōmiṟkup puṟappaṭṭāṉ.
antōṇimītu, avaṉ kātal neñciṉmītu tāṉ āṭci celuttiṉum, atē neñciṟku iṉṉoru pakuti vīrappakuti uṇṭeṉṟum, atit tāṉ uṟaṅka vikka muṭiyumēyaṉṟi oḻikka muṭiyāteṉṟum, atu viḻittu viṭṭāl avaṉit tāṉ aṭakkiyāḷa muṭiyāteṉṟum kiḷiyōppātrā naṉku aṟivāḷ. ippōtu avaṉ vīra uru eṭuttu viṭṭapaṭiyāl avaḷ avaṉ muṉ kāṟṟilāṭum māntaḷireṉa naṭuṅkiṉāḷ. avaḷ āṇippaṭi, avaḷ cēṭiyar avaḷukku uṭal nalamilliyeṉṟum, avaḷ ciṉaṅkoṇṭuḷḷāḷ eṉṟum, palavāṟu pēcip pārttum avaṉ pōvatilēyē muṉintu niṉṟami kaṇṭu avaṉiṭamiruntu ūṭit taṉ aṟi ceṉṟu, avaṉip pirinta tuyar poṟātu pitaṟṟi appirivu nāṭkaḷiṉ ovvoru noṭiyiyum ovvoru ciṟiya ūḻiyāka eṇṇik kaḻittāḷ.
pāmpiyāl alikkaḻikkappaṭṭu niṉṟa sīsarum leppiṭasum antōṇiyi rōmil kaṇṭatē, kāṭṭil alintu paciyālum viṭāyālum varuntiya makkaḷ muṉ vēṭacciṟārum ciṟumiyarum iṉmukattuṭaṉ tēṉum tiṉimāvum ūṉum koṇṭu vantāleppaṭiyō appaṭik kaḷippuṟṟaṉar. ituvari oruvarukkoruvar ceyta tīmikaḷip paṟṟikkūṭa vaṟpuṟuttip pēcātu tōḻarkaḷuṭaṉ kuttalākap pēcumuṟiyil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiviṭṭup piṉ iṇakkamāka oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar kūṭik kulāvalāyiṉar. ivvoṟṟumi neṭunāḷ nilittiruntālaṉṟit taṉ kāriyaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟa eṉṟaṟinta akṭēviyas sīsar, taṉ naṇparkaḷi antōṇiyiṉ maṉappāṅkaṟintu taṉ oṉṟuviṭṭa uṭaṉpiṟantāḷākiya akṭēviyāvi avaṉ maṇakkumpaṭi tūṇṭac ceytāṉ.
antōṇiyiṉ teḷinta maṉaccāṉṟu ippōtu kiḷiyōppātrāvuṭaṉ tāṉ naṭattiya cōmpal vāḻvi veṟuttatākaliṉ, iṉit tāṉ nal uṇarvuṭiyavaṉāy āṇmi vāḻvu vāḻa ittakiya maṇam utavum eṉṟu niṉittāṉ. itaṟkēṟpa akṭēviyā eḷiya kuṇamum uyarnta kaṭamiyuṇarvum uṭiyavaḷāyc cūtuvātaṟṟiruntamiyāl, avaḷ aṉṟu avaṉatu ippirivu maṉappāṉmiyil mikavum uyarntavaḷāyk kāṇappaṭṭāḷ. ākavē virivil av viruvariyum sīsar maṇamuṭittu vittuviṭṭāṉ.
avarkaḷum cilanāṭkaḷ kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ māyavali yiṉiṉṟum, rōmiṉ araciyal cikkalkaḷiṉiṉṟum vilaki niṉṟu tūya maṇavāḻkki yilīṭupaṭṭaṉar.
rōmil mūvarum mīṇṭum oruṅku cērntatum iṇakkamaṭintatum pāmpiyiṉ valimiyik kuṟittaṉa. avarkaḷi iṉi etirppatu aritu eṉṟu kaṇṭu avaṉ avarkaḷuṭaṉ uṟavu koṇṭuviṭa eṇṇiṉāṉ. ataṟku avarkaḷum uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉar. uṭaṉpaṭavē pāmpi taṉṉuṭaṉ kappalil vittu viruntuṇṇa mūvariyum varumāṟu aḻittāṉ. mukkaṉikaḷum tēṉum pālum ciṟṟuṇṭikaḷum avarkaḷ naṭpiṟkaṟikuṟikaḷāka vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉa. iṉip pōrāṭṭamiṉṟi nālvarum tammuḷ oruvariyoruvar caricamamākak koṇṭu vāḻvateṉṟum, oruvar paṅkil oruvar pukuvatilli eṉṟum uṟuti kūṟiyapiṉ avarkaḷ pirintaṉar.
āṉāl, annāṉku uḷḷaṅkaḷilum oṉṟu maṭṭum innaṭpi oru mēluṟiyākak koṇṭu uḷḷūra nañcik kalantu oḷintu vittuk koṇṭiruntatu. atuvē sīsaratu uḷḷamākum. ulaki muṟṟilum tāṉē āḷavēṇṭumeṉṉum ōr eṇṇattiyum ataṟkāṉa cūḻccikaḷiyum tantiraṅ kaḷiyum allāmal, naṭpu, kātal, nampikki ākiya ittakiya eṇṇaṅkaḷ etaṟkum avaṉ atil iṭaṅkoṭukkavilli. ākavē pāmpi, avarkaḷit tam viruntiṉar eṉṟeṇṇik kappalil avarkaḷ taṉṉiṭam vantu cikkiyapōtu avarkaḷic ciṟipiṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟiya taṉ tuṇittalivaṉ moḻiyic ciṉantu kaṇṭikkavum, leppiṭasum antōṇiyum tam nalaṉi maṟantu akṭēviyas sīsarukku uyarvum opparavum nalkavum, akṭēviyas oruvaṉ maṭṭum neñcil vañcaṅ koṇṭu avarkaḷaṉivariyum pirittu aḻikkum eṇṇaṅ koṇṭavaṉāyiruntāṉ.
ūḻ ivvakiyil akṭēviyasukkup pērutavi purintatu. mutalāvatākap pāmpi taṟceyalākat taṉ paṇiyāḷ oruvaṉ kiyāl koliyuṇṭāṉ. atik kēṭṭatum iṉi leppiṭas utavi vēṇṭuvatilli eṉṟu kaṇṭu akṭēviyas, avaṉ pāmpiyuṭaṉ maṟivāka eḻuttup pōkkuravu vittiruntāṉ eṉṟu poyk kuṟṟañ cāṭṭiṉāṉ. akṭēviyasiṭam nampikki vittu avaṉi rōmiṉ talivaṉ eṉṟē koṇṭa peruntaṉmiyum nāṭṭup paṟṟum mikka leppiṭas, tāṉ taṉ nāṭṭut talivaṉatu nampikkiyiyiḻantatākak kaṇṭatē tāṉākavē taṉniliyi viṭṭu avaṉiṭam ciṟiyāḷiyāka vantu niṉṟāṉ. akṭēviyas apperuntaki leppiṭasip pārāṭṭuvataṟku māṟāka avaṉ kuṟṟatti vaṟpuṟutti avaṉik koli ceytāṉ.
rōmap pēraraciṉ paṅkāḷikaḷuḷ ippōtu akṭēviyasukku etiriyāka mīntiruntavaṉ antōṇi oruvaṉē. taṉatu mūṉṟu etirikaḷilum vīrattālum tiṟaṉālum taṉakkuppēriṭar tarattakka etiri avaṉē eṉpatu akṭēviyasukkut teriyum. ataṉāl avaṉ taṉ taṅki mūlam avaṉip piṇittu vikka eṇṇiṉāṉ. āṉāl ippōtu pāmpiyum leppiṭasum pōṉapiṉ, atuvum avaṉ kiḷiyōp pātrāviṉ valiyil piṇippuṇṭirukkiyil avaṉi etirppatu avvaḷavu kaṭumiyāṉataṉṟu eṉṟuṅ kaṇṭāṉ. antōṇiyum taṉatu maṉaccāṉṟukku iṭaṅkoṭutta aḷavē akṭēviyāvukku iṇakka muṭiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ. uḷḷuṇarcciyum iṉpa viḻivum ēṟpaṭṭatē illi; avaḷ avaṉukkuk karumpiṉ naṟiya cāṟṟiṉiṉṟu piritteṭukkap peṟṟa veḷḷiya cakkipōlat tōṉṟiṉāḷ. akṭēviyā ulakameccum maṉiviyātaṟ kuriyavaḷēyaṉṟit taṉ uḷḷaṅ kavarntavaḷ allaḷ eṉṟu avaṉ kaṇṭu kiḷiyōppātrā irunta iṭam nōkkalāṉāṉ.
3. pirivum, pirintavarkūṭalum
antōṇi taṉṉuṭaṉ iruntapoḻutu avaṉ taṉ kātalukku aṭimi eṉa niṉittirunta kiḷiyōppātrā, ippōtu uṇmiyil tāṉē avaṉ kātalukku aṭimi eṉpati uṇarntāḷ. avaḷ uṭalum uyirum ippōtu iṭiviṭātu avaṉatu toṭarpu, avaṉ tōṟṟam, avaṉ moḻikaḷ ivaṟṟilēyē nāṭṭamāy niṉṟaṉa. ūṇilum uṭiyilum āṭal pāṭalkaḷilum avaḷ koṇṭa viruppu aṉittum antōṇi eṉṉum oru poruḷāl varum iṉpattiṟkuk karuvikaḷāka maṭṭumē ippōtu tōṉṟiṉa. avaḷ cēṭiyār evvaḷavu vēṇṭiṉum avaḷ avaṟṟalō piṟa aracē kāriyaṅkaḷilō maṉañ celuttāmal, antōṇiyiyē niṉittu niṉittup perumūccu viṭṭa vaṇṇam iruntāḷ.
rōmil antōṇi enniliyil irukkiṟāṉ eṉṉeṉṉa ceykiṟāṉ eṉpatiyaṟiya, kiḷiyōppātrā maṟimukamāka oṟṟari aṉuppiyiruntāḷ. avarkaḷ mūlamāka vanta ceytikaḷ avaḷ maṉattip piṉṉum mikutiyākak kuḻappiṉa. antōṇi taṉ kātal taḷiyiṉiṉṟu viṭupaṭṭa taṉṟi, araciyaluḷ muḻu maṉattuṭaṉ īṭupaṭṭu mūvaruṭaṉ ottuḻittatu avaḷ maṉatti vāḷpōl īrttatu, ataṉpiṉ, avaṉ akṭēviyāvi maṇantāṉ eṉṟa ceytiyik kēṭkavē, avaḷaṭinta ciṉattiṟkum tuyarukkum elliyilli.
acceyti koṇṭu vanta oṟṟaṉiyē mutalil avaḷ ciṉantu pāyntu kolla iruntāḷ. avaḷ paṇiyāṭkaḷuḷ talivaṉāṉa eṉōpārpas avaḷukkut tēṟutal kūṟi avaḷi amitippaṭuttiṉāṉ. antōṇi akṭēviyāvi maṇantatu uṇmiyil sīsaruṭaṉ avaṉip piṇippataṉṟu, muraṇac ceyvatēyām eṉṟu avaṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉāṉ. atōṭu akṭēviyā aḻakaṟṟavaḷ eṉṟum, antōṇiyiṉ uḷḷak kiḷarccikku oru noṭiyum īṭu celuttakkūṭātavaḷ eṉṟum avaṉ kūṟavē, avaḷ taṉ tuyari maṟantu antōṇiyi mīṇṭum taṉ pakkam iḻukkum vaḻi yāteṉa ārāyalāṉāḷ.
iccamayam akṭēviyā taṉ kaṇavaṉi viṭṭu akaṉṟu taṉ tamiyaṉip pārkkac celkiṟāḷ eṉṟu ceyti vantatu.
sīsar ippōtu nēraṭiyāka antōṇiyik kuṟi kūṟavum, avaṉ naṇparkaḷic ciṟipaṭutti oṟukkavum toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. ivaṟṟik kēṭṭum, leppiṭasiṉiṭam sīsar naṭantukoṇṭa vakiyi aṟintum antōṇi avaṉmītu cīṟṟaṅkoṇṭu ataṉi akṭēviyāviṉiṭamum kāṭṭiṉāṉ. kaṇavaṉiyum tamiyaṉiyum oruṅkē paṟṟiniṉṟa akṭēviyā tamiyaṉi nērē ceṉṟu tirutta eṇṇi rōm nakar ceṉṟāḷ.
leppiṭasiṉ naṭpiyum peruntaṉmiyiyum pārāta sīsar antōṇiyiṉ uṟaviyum taṅkiyiṉ nalvāḻviyumkūṭa eṇṇip pārāṉ eṉpatu etirppārkkat takkatēyaṉṟō? avaṉ taṅki vāy tiṟakkum muṉṉākavē, avaḷi antōṇi rōmap pēraraciṉ paṅkāḷiyākiya taṉ maṉiviyeṉṟa muṟiyiṉ virutukaḷuṭaṉum mēḷatāḷaṅkaḷuṭaṉum aṉuppāmal paṇip peṇ pōlat taṉiyē aṉuppiṉāṉ eṉṟu avaṉmītu cīṟiṉāṉ. antōṇi mītu inta avamatippiṟkākap paḻivāṅkuva teṉakkūṟi, avaṉ taṉ pakimikkup putiya cākkuk kaṇṭāṉ. iṅṅaṉam mittuṉar iruvariyum oṉṟu cērkkum eṇṇattuṭaṉ vanta akṭēviyā avarkaḷip pirittu vikkavē utaviyavaḷ pōlāṉāḷ.
akṭēviyā pōṉatē, antōṇi tāṉ ituvari valiyuṟutti mēṟkoṇṭirunta ceyaṟkiyāṇmiyākiya pōrviyik kiḻittu eṟintuviṭṭu, muṉṉiṉum paṉmaṭaṅku ārvattuṭaṉ virintu kiḷiyōp pātrāviṭam ceṉṟu cērntāṉ.
“pirintavar kūṭiṉāl pēcavum vēṇṭumō?” nīriṉṟi  veṭitta nilattil maḻittuḷi viḻuntālaṉṉa avaṉ varavu avaḷ neñciṉ tuṭippiyum ēkkattiyum akaṟṟi avaḷi iṉpa ulakēḻiṉum mēlām tuṟakka ulaku eytuvittatu.
tāṉiṉṟi antōṇi vāḻiṉumkūṭa iṉittāṉ antōṇiyiṉṟi vāḻa muṭiyāteṉak kiḷiyōppātrā kaṇṭāḷ. ākavē avaḷ atumutal, eṅkē antōṇi iyaṟki vīra uru eṭuttut taṉṉiviṭṭu akaṉṟu viṭuvāṉō eṉṟu añcalāṉāḷ. ataṉ payaṉāka avaṉatu vīra uṇarcci eḻātapaṭi ōyātu kātal uṇarcaciyit tūṇṭi iṭiviṭā vēliyāyiṟṟu. maṟantuṅkūṭa avaṉ akkōṭṭiyi viṭṭakala eṇṇātapaṭi ceyvatil avaḷ taṉ oppaṟṟa tiṟaṉiyum māyavitti kaḷiyum kāṭṭalāṉāḷ.
peṇmiyē uruveṭuttu vantatupōṉṟa ap peṇṇaracikku itu mikak kaṭumiyāṉa paṇiyumaṉṟu. kūṭaliṉ uyarnili ūṭalē eṉpatiyuṇarntu, avaḷ antōṇiyi oru camayam taṉ pakkam īrppatum aṭutta kaṇamē avaṉi veṟuttut taḷḷuvatum pōl naṭittu, avaṉ ārvattip paṉmaṭaṅku mikippaṭuttiṉāḷ. avaḷ varavum celavum, avaḷ viruppum veṟuppum, pukaḻcciyum ikaḻcciyum, nakippum ciṉamum, māṟi māṟi oḷiyum niḻalum pōlavum, uṭalum uyirum pōlavum, pakalum iravum pōlavum niṉṟu, avaṉ vīramum aṟivum avaṉiṭam varuvataṟku ōyvu tarātapaṭi avaṉ maṉattit tākki urimippaṭuttiṉa.
āyiṉ, eṉṉa iruntālum avaḷum peṇtāṉē! taṉatu immuyaṟciyiṉāl taṉ uyirukkum taṉ kātalaṉ vāḻkkikkum uli vikkiṟōmeṉṟu avaḷ aṟintilaḷ! jūliyas sīsaratu matippiṟkum pāmpiyiṉ accattiṟkum iṭamāṉa antōṇi, taṉ kātal valippaṭṭu akṭēviyas sīsar pōṉṟa mōḻiyum pāyum ēḻiyāy viṭṭāṉ eṉṟu avaḷ aṟintilaḷ!
antōṇi ekiptil kiḷiyōppātrāvuṭaṉum, avar cēṭiyar pēṭiyaruṭaṉum cūtāṭiyum, avaḷuṭaṉ poyki nīrāṭiyum, pūmpantāṭiyum, poṟpaṭaku ūrntum, āṭal pāṭalkaḷ kēḷikkikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil kalantum nāḷ pōkki vantāṉ. atēcamayam avaṉ naṭimuṟikaḷ muṟṟilum oṟṟar mūlam aṟintu vanta akṭēviyas itutāṉ camayameṉṟu kaṇṭu antōṇiyiṉ naṇparkaḷi ovvoruvarāka muṟiyaṭittu aḻittum, avaṉ paṅkiluḷḷa nāṭukaḷi veṉṟu kikkoṇṭum vantāṉ.  avaṟṟik kātāṟ kēṭṭum maṉattāl koḷḷāta vaṉāy antōṇi cilanāḷ kaṭattiṉāṉ. piṉ “varaṭṭum, avaṉ eṅkē pōkiṟāṉ. oru ki pārkkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu uṟukki nāḷ pōkkiṉāṉ. taṉ paṭittalivarkaḷiṭam, “iṉṟu puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟetirppōm; nāḷi puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟetirppōm” eṉṟu kūṟi muyaṟciyēyiṉṟip palanāḷ kaḻittaṉaṉ. iṅṅaṉamāka ekiptum kīḻ nāṭukaḷum nīṅkalāka ellām sīsar vacamāyiṉa.
4. kātalukku vīram paliyātal
rōmap pēraraciṉ kuḻappatti viṭa ippōtu antōṇiyiṉ maṉattiluḷḷa kuḻappamē mikap peritāyiruntatu. sīsariṉa ciṟumiyi - avaṉatu naṉṟikoṉṟa taṉatti - avaṉ taṉṉi avamatitta amatippi - eṇṇumpōtellām avaṉ tōḷkaḷ tuṭittaṉa; avaṉ mīcikaḷ paṭapaṭattaṉa. appōtu avaṉ intak kiḷiyōppātrāviyum avaḷatu ekiptiyum ciṉṉāpiṉṉap paṭuttiviṭuvōmā eṉṟeḻuvāṉ. aṭutta noṭiyē ak kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ uruvam - nakiyum cīṟṟamum māṟi māṟi vantu naṭaṉamiṭum. avaḷ mukam mayilum nāṇa aṉṉamum piṉṉiṭa naṭantuvarum avaḷatu oyyāra naṭi - tēṉiṉumiṉiya teviṭṭā amirtāṉa avaḷ kuyilici moḻikaḷ - ākiya ivi avaṉ maṉattiriyil vantu tavaḻum. appōtu avaṉ taṉ vīrattiyum taṉ aṟiviyum iḻantu ‘ivvuruviṉ muṉ rōmap pēraraciyum eṉ vāḻviyuṅ kūṭap palikoṭutta tayaṅkēṉ” eṉpāṉ.
iṟutiyil sīsar maṟṟa nāṭukaḷ aṉittiyum veṉṟu taṉṉi etirkka ekiptu nōkki varukiṟāṉ eṉṟu kēḷvip paṭṭatum antōṇi, mēṟpaṇiyāḷaṉ varavu kēṭṭu eḻum kuṭiveṟippaṭṭa paṭiñaṉ pōl eḻuntāṉ. taṉatu nāṭākiya ekiptukkē iṭiyūṟu vantatu kaṇṭu kiḷiyōppātrāvum antōṇiyiṉ kātiṟ pukikku naṟuntūḷiṭum vēliyi viṭṭu eḻuntāḷ. virivil amiccarkaḷukkum paṭit talivar kaḷukkum uttaravukaḷ paṟantaṉa. pēṭiyarum cēṭiyarum añci akala niṟka vīraratu pōr avi kūṭik kalantatu.
antōṇiyi viṭṭakala virumpāmal kiḷiyōppātrā tāṉum pōril taṉ paṭikkut taliviyāy varavēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉāḷ. antōṇiyiṉ amiccaṉum naṇpaṉumāṉa eṉōpārpas,  peṇṭir pōrkkaḷam celvatu naṉṟaṉṟu eṉṟum, atilum kiḷiyōppātrā antōṇikkuk kāṟkaṭṭāyiruppāḷ eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. kiḷiyōppātrā itaṉi ēṟkātu avaṉi vāyaṭakkiṉāḷ.
kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ ippiḻikaḷāl vanta tīṅkiyaṉṟi vēṟevvakiyilum veṟṟikkuriya muḻuvaṉmi antōṇi pakkamē iruntatu. avaṉatu paṭi avvaḷavu vīramum naṟpayiṟciyumuṭiyatu; aḵtu avaṉiṭam niṟinta paṟṟuṭiyatu; avaṉukkāka uyiri oru poruṭṭāka matiyātu pōriṭum iyalpuṭiyatu. antōṇiyum oppaṟṟa vīraṉ; taṉ paṭikaḷi veṟṟimēl veṟṟiyāka niṉṟu naṭattiya nērilāt talivaṉ; sīsariṉ paṭi antōṇiyiṉ paṭikku īṭuṭiyatum vīramuṭiyatumaṉṟu; sīsarum vīrattilum talimit tiṟattilum antōṇikku īṭuṭiyatum vīramuṭiyatumaṉṟu; sīsarum vīrattilum talimit tiṟattilum antōṇikku īṭu ākāṉ.
kaṭaṟpaṭi vakiyil nilimi itaṟku nērmāṟāṉatu. sīsar paṭi pāmapiyāl payiṟci tarappaṭṭatu; antōṇiyiṉ kappaṟpaṭiyō ekiptu araci ulāppōkum camayam aḻakiṟkāka uṭaṉcella ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ākavē, nilattaṟ pōr purivatu antōṇikkē nallatu eṉṟum kaṭaliṟ pōr purivatē taṉakku nallatu eṉṟum sīsarukku naṉku pulappaṭṭatu. eṅkē antōṇi taṉṉi nilattil tākkiviṭuvāṉō eṉṟu avaṉukku uḷḷūra accamāyiruntatu.
kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ pōkku itilum sīsarukku naṉmiyākavē muṭintatu. eṉōpārpas antōṇiyiṉ kaṭaṟpaṭiyi viṭa nilappaṭiyē veṟṟiyuṭaṉ pōrpuriyat takkatu eṉṟapōtu, avaḷ avaṉ taṉ nāṭṭiṉ kaṭaṟpaṭiyik kuṟi kūṟiṉāṉ eṉakkoṇṭu itilum avaṉ kūṟiyataṟku nērmāṟāka antōṇiyit tūṇṭiṉāḷ. ‘tiyal coṟ kēḷēl’ eṉṉum mūturiyi mīṟi avaṉum avaḷ colvatiyē mēṟkoṇṭu kaṭalilēyē caṇṭi ceyyumāṟu eṉōpārpasukkuk kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ.
eṉōpārpas kūṟiyapaṭiyē ekiptiyak kaṭaṟpaṭi sīsaratu paṭiyuṭaṉ cila maṇi nēraṅkūṭa niṟka āṟṟāmal muṟintu piṉṉaṭintōṭiyatu. kiḷiyōppātrāvum uṭaṉ tāṉē naṭunaṭuṅkit  taṉ kappaliyum avaṟṟuṭaṉ ōṭumpaṭi āṇiyiṭṭāḷ; pāvam! tāṉ pōvatu antōṇikku uyir pōvatu pōl irukkum eṉpati avaḷ maṟantāḷ.
irupaṭiyum neruṅkip pōrpuriyum vēḷiyil antōṇiyiṉ kaṇkaḷ taṟceyalākak kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ pakkamākac cāya appōtu avaṉ avaḷ paṭikaḷ uṭintōṭuvatiyum avaḷum uṭaṉōṭu vatiyum kaṇṭāṉ. kaṇṭatē avaṉ pōrum maṟantāṉ. puviyum maṟantāṉ; pōrvīrar niliyiyum maṟantu, ōṭukiṉṟa tāyppacuvip piṉpaṟṟum kaṉṟippōla avaḷip piṉpaṟṟi ōṭiṉāṉ.
puṟaṅkoṭā vīraṉākiya avaṉ maṉattil kātaṟpēyiruntu uyiriṉum mikka māṉattiyum uṇṭateṉṉal vēṇṭum!
talivaṉ pōṉapiṉ paṭi eṉṉa ceyyum? atu nālāpakkamum citaṟi sīsaratu paṭiyiṉ vāḷukkum, ampumārikkum iriyākic cīrkulivuṟṟatu. maṉañ ceṉṟa vaḻiyum pulaṉ ceṉṟa vaḻiyum cellum antēṇiyip pōlātu ammaṉap pōkkiyum ulakap pōkkiyum kāṟṟiyum pūtaṅkaḷiyum kūṭat tāṉ karutiya aruṅkāhyat tiṟkut tuṇiyākum paṭi taṉ kūr aṟivāl iṇikkam oppaṟṟa cūḻcciyāḷaṉākiya akṭēviyas sīsar aṉṟu ūḻ eṇṇam aliyiṉ ucciyil mitantu veṟṟi vīraṉāy viḷaṅkiṉāṉ.
5.iṟutip pōr
uṇarcci vacappaṭṭup pōriviṭṭu ōṭiya antōṇi ekiptu ceṉṟatum taṉ vīrar aṭinta paṭutōlviyiyum aḻiviyum kēṭṭu maṉamurukiṉāṉ. appōtutāṉ avaṉukkut tāṉ aṭinta tīṅkiṉ muḻu vaṉmiyum pulappaṭṭatu. paṭavē iḵtaṉittiṟkum ētuvāṉa kiḷiyōppātrāviṉ mītu avaṉ cīṟi viḻuntāṉ. aṉṟu avaṉ kaṇkaḷukku avaḷ kuṟṟaṅkaḷ terintaṉa eṉṟu maṭṭumalla; akkuṟṟaṅkaḷ peritākkappaṭṭum, pala tappeṇṇaṅkaḷ cērntum, avaḷ uṭalēpōl avaḷ uḷḷamum avaṉ pārvikkuk karumiyākat tōṟṟiṉa. ākavē, avaḷum taṉṉip pōlavē uṇarcci vacappaṭṭuttāṉ ittaṉi aḻivukaḷiyum uṇṭu paṇṇiṉāḷ eṉṟu koḷvataṟku māṟāka, avaḷ vēṇṭumeṉṟē taṉnalattiṉāl tūṇṭappeṟṟa sīsarukku uṭantiyāy niṉṟu taṉṉik kaviḻkkat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ eṉṟu cīṟiṉāṉ.
iṉmoḻiyāl mukamalarum meṉmalarkaḷākiya naṅkiyar cevikaḷil vaṉmoḻikaḷ pukuntāl avarkaḷ tuṉpattik kēṭpāṉēṉ! avaṉatu cīṟṟattāl avaṉ kātali iḻantu viṭṭōmē eṉṟa accam orupuṟam; avaṉ taṉṉik kolvatākap pāynteḻumpōtu eṅkē tāṉ avaṉ kātaliyum uyiriyum oruṅkē iḻakka nēriṭumō eṉṟa accam orupuṟamāka niṉṟu avaḷi vāṭṭiṉa.
eppaṭiyum taṉ uyirik kāttuviṭṭāl avaṉ ciṉantaṇittapiṉ avaṉ tappeṇṇaṅkaḷi māṟṟi viṭalām eṉṟu niṉittu avaḷ ōṭit taṉ irukkiyaṟiyiṟ ceṉṟoḷintu koṇṭāḷ.
appuṭiyum antōṇi amiyāmal taṉṉik kollat tēṭukiṟāpa eṉṟu  kēṭṭa kiḷiyōppātrā tāṉ iṟantu viṭṭatākavē avaṉiṭam kūṟiviṭumpaṭi āṇiyiṭṭāḷ. ittantiram muṟṟilum veṟṟi peṟṟatu.
avaḷ iṟantāḷ eṉṟu kēṭṭatu antōṇiyiṉ cīṟṟamellām paṭameṭutta pāmpu mantirattilaṭaṅkuvatu pōla aṭaṅki oṭuṅkiṟṟu. avaḷ ettakiyavaḷ āyiṉum avaḷillā ulakil taṉakku ettakiya viruppukkum iṭaṉilliyeṉṟu kaṇṭu avaṉum cākat tuṇintu viṭṭāṉ. itaṉiyaṟintum cēṭiyār avaṉit tēṟṟi avaḷ uṇmiyil iṟakkavilli eṉṟu kūṟiṉar.
avarkaḷ kūṟi vāymūṭumuṉ kiḷiyōppātrāvē nēril vantu avaṉ kālil vīḻntu, “nāṉ taṅkaḷ vāḻvit tolittavaḷ; taṅkaḷ perumikkum taṅkaḷ kātalukkum ovvātavaḷ; eṉṉi ataṟkāka eppaṭit taṇṭittālum cari; tāṅkaḷ kāṭṭum veṟuppiyum cīṟṟattiyum maṭṭumē kaṇṭu añcukiṟēṉ. taṅkaḷ kātalukkāka uyir viṭuvatāyiṉ makiḻcciyuṭaṉ iṟappēṉ” eṉṟāḷ.
antōṇi avaḷit taṉ nīṇṭa kikaḷāl vāri eṭuttaṇittu, ‘eṉ arumporuḷē! niṉ aṉpi nōkka ippēraracum, ivvulakuntāṉ eṉṉa viliyuṭiyatu! niṉ oru puṉmuṟuvalukkāka iṉṉum ēḻumuṟi ivvulakatti veṉṟu veṉṟiḻakka nēriṉum poruṭpaṭuttēṉ. nī kavali koḷḷa vēṇṭā’’ eṉṟāṉ.
ippōtu kiḷiyōppātrāvukkut taṉ kātal pōyiṟṟeṉa cīṟṟam maṟintuviṭṭatu; atōṭu antōṇiyō viṟkum taṉ kiḷiyōppātrā pōyiṉaḷ eṉṟa ventuyar akaṉṟatu. muṉpōl avaḷatu matimukamum, tuṇikkaraṅkaḷum, iṉmoḻikaḷum avaṉukku uyirum ūkkamum aḷittaṉa. avaṉ avaḷ mukatti uṟṟunōkkiya vaṇṇam, “ām, immukattil kātal oḷiyuḷḷavum eṉ uḷḷattil vīra oḷikkuk kuṟivilli. uṉ peyarāl-uṉ kātaliṉ peyarāl-iṉṉum orumuṟi ivvulaki velvēṉ” eṉṟeḻuntāṉ.
antōṇiyi niḻalpōl toṭarntu uṭaluṟuppuk kaḷō eṉṉumpaṭi māṟupāṭiṉṟi avaṉ karuttaṟintuviya avaṉ vīrarum, tuṇit talivarum itukāṟum avaṉ kātaṟ perumpuyalālum cīṟṟattālum taṉ nili iḻantu niṟpatukaṇṭu varuntic ceyaliḻantu niṉṟaṉar. ippōtu avaṉ mukattē vīrakkaḷiyik kaṇṭatumē, “iṉit tayakkam vēṇṭā, em talivar eḻuntār; iṉiyum veṟṟi namatē. ulakam namatē” eṉṟu āravārittu eḻuntaṉar.
maṟunāḷē akṭēviyas paṭi ekiptil vantiṟaṅkiyatu. antōṇiyum avaṉ vīrarkaḷum ataṉuṭaṉ kikalantu tākkiṉar. akṭēviyas paṭi antōṇiyiṉ paṭiyiviṭa evvaḷavō periyatāyiruntum, antōṇiyiṉ āṟṟalukkum avaṉ vīrararkaḷiṉ tuṇiccalukkum āṟṟamāṭṭātu piṉṉiṭintōṭiṟṟu, sīsar taṉ muḻuttiṟaṉiyum kāṭṭi avarkaḷit taṉ kiyāṭkaḷ mūlam cāṭṭiyāl aṭittu āṭṭuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷip pōl ellippuṟattil tiraṭṭic cērkkavilliyāṉāl, avarkaḷ mīṭka muṭiyātapaṭi citaṟiyē iruppar.
āṇmimikka antōṇiyiṉ vīrar ivveṟṟiyāl piṉṉum ūkkam aṭintaṉar. avveṟṟiyit toṭarntu sīsariṉ paṭikaḷi rōm varikkum turattiyōṭṭi avaṉi aḻikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu antōṇiyiṉ tuṇit talivaṉ maṉṟāṭiṉāṉ. āṉāl antōṇiyiṉ iyaṟkik cōmpalum iṉpaviḻivum ivviṉṟiyamiyāk kaṭamiyic ceyyak kālantāḻttiṉa. veṟṟiyik koṇṭāṭum vakiyil taṉ poruṭkuvi muṟṟilum taṉ vīrararkaḷukku vāri iṟittum avarkaḷuṭaṉ uṇṭāṭṭayarntum avaṉ poḻutu pōkkiṉāṉ. itaṉik kaṇṭum taṉ naṉmoḻikaḷi avaṉ puṟakkaṇittāl ciṉaṅkoṇṭum attuṇit talivaṉ aṉṟiravē avaṉ etiriyiṉ pakkam ceṉṟu cērntu koṇṭāṉ.
icceytiyik kēṭṭa antōṇi avaṉiṭam eḷḷaḷavum cīṟṟaṅ koḷḷātu maṟṟa vīrarkaḷiṭamum “avaṉip pōlavē kālanilik koppa  nīṅkaḷ naṭantu koḷvatē eṉakku viruppam” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. atōṭu sīsar pakkam ceṉṟavaṉ taṉatu āttirattil viṭṭuppōṉa poruḷkaḷi avaṉiṭamē cērkkumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ. uṇmiyil antōṇiyiṭam nēcam koṇṭu cīṟṟattālēyē etirk kaṭciyil ceṉṟu cērnta attuṇit talivaṉ itukēṭṭut taṉ naṉṟiyiṉmiyiyum taṉ talivaṉ peruntaṉmiyum niṉittuk kaṇṇīrukuttāṉ.
antōṇiyum avaṉ vīrarum irunta nilimiyiṉi oṟṟar mūlam aṟinta sīsar taṉ paṭiyi iravōṭiravākat tiraṭṭik kuṭi mayakkattilum, tuyil mayakkattilum, tammi maṟantirunta antōṇiyiṉ paṭivīrar mītu pāyntāṉ. koñca nērattiṟkuḷ antōṇiyiṉ paṭi irunta iṭam teriyātu aḻintoḻintatu. eñcila cila vīrarum, antōṇiyum, kiḷiyōppātrāvum arumpāṭupaṭṭut tappiyōṭit tam araṇmaṉi vantu cērntaṉar.
6. muṭivu
vīraruḷḷum tuṇittalivaruḷḷum palar iṉṉum antōṇiyi nīṅkātu piṉtoṭarntu vantaṉar. cettālum avaṉuṭaṉ cāvativiṭat tamakku uyarnta pēṟu vēṟilli eṉṟē avarkaḷ niṉittaṉar. āṉāl antōṇi taṉṉāl ituvari avarkaḷ keṭṭatu pōtum eṉṟu niṉittut taṉ poruṭkuviyaṉittum, tuṇimaṇiyaṉittum avarkaṭkē koṭuttu “iviyaṉittum uṅkaḷ veṟṟiyāl vanta poruḷkaḷē. eṉ aṟiyāmiyāl iḻantavi pōka  mītattiyēṉum uṅkaḷiṭam cērkkiṟēṉ. eṭuttukkoṇṭu naccumaramākiya eṉṉi viṭṭakalvīr! eṉakku nīṅkaḷ ceyyum kaṭamiyi eṉatu viruppattiṉum ettaṉiyō maṭaṅku nīṅkaḷ ceytu tīrttu viṭṭapaṭiyāl iṉi uṅkaḷiṭam ettakiya kaṭamiyiyum nāṉ etirpārkkavilli. eṉ peyar colvatē iṉit tīṅku viḷivikkumātalāl ataṉi maṟantu kālattiṟkēṟpa naṭantu koḷḷuṅkaḷ. eṉakku vēṇṭiya tellām eṉ naṇparkaḷākiya uṅkaḷ nalvāḻvē” eṉṟāṉ.
taṉ veṟṟi nāḷ muḻumiyum kātalukkāka uyirip paliyiṭṭa ipperunta takiyāḷaṉ, tōlviyilum naṭpiṟ kākat taṉṉip paliyiṭuvatik kaṇṭu vīraṉivarum kaṇṇī rukuttaṉar. avaṉi viṭṭuccella avarkaḷ evarum virumpa villiyāyiṉum avaṉ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum vaṟpuṟuttiyataṉ pēril ovvoruvarum piriyā viṭi peṟṟuk kāl muṉṉum maṉam piṉṉum iḻukka avaṉi viṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉar.
akṭēviyas sīsar ippōtu ulakap pēraracaṉāka viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. ekiptum avaṉ kālaṭiyil paṭṭuviṭṭatu. āṉāl, avaṉ antōṇiyirukkumaḷavum taṉnili uṟutiyuṭittaṉṟu eṉ aṟintu avaṉiyum avaṉukku uṭantiyāyirunta kiḷiyōppātrāviyum uyiruṭaṉ kippaṟṟic ciṟiyuḷ vikka eṇṇiṉāṉ. āṉāl, iṟakkum taṟuvāyilum ciṅkavēṟṟi makkaḷ aṇuka añcuvatupōla avaṉiyum kiḷiyōppātrāviyum nēril aṇuka añcit tūtar mūlam akṭēviyas avarkaḷ maṉaniliyaṟintu varumpaṭi ēviṉāṉ.
antōṇi taṉakkup parivu vēṇṭiyatilli eṉṟum taṉ tuṇivarkaḷiyum kiḷiyōppātrāviyum maṭṭum matippuṭaṉ naṭattiṉāl pōtum eṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ. piṉ avaṉ kiḷiyōppātrāvi nōkki iṉit taṉṉi viṭṭuviṭṭu sīsari nayantu nalvāḻvu peṟavatē avaḷukku nallatu; taṉakkum atu maṉattiṟkukantateṉa vēṇṭiṉāṉ.
itukāṟum peṇmiyiṉ meṉmiyiyē kāṭṭiya kiḷiyōppātrā iccoṟ kēṭṭatē araciyiṉ perumitat tōṟṟaṅkoṇṭu tali nimirntu sīsar tūtaṉi nōkki, araci kiḷiyōppātrā antōṇi oruvaṉ muṉ maṭṭumē peṇ āvaḷ; piṟarukku araciyēyāvāḷ, araci eṉṟa niliyil vāḻa muṭiyavilliyāyiṉ araciyākavē cāva aṟivāḷ” eṉṟu kūṟi aṉuppiviṭṭup piṉ antōṇi pakkamākat tirumpi, “eṉ aracē! nīṅkaḷ kūṭa eṉ kātali iṅṅaṉam paḻikkap poṟukkēṉ, antō! nāṉ kariya ekiptiyariṭip piṟantu jūliyas sīsar mutaliya palaruṭaṉ uṟavāṭiyati niṉittā laṉṟō eṉ antōṇi! eṉṉi ivvaḷavu iḻivāka matikka iṭamēṟpaṭṭatu! eṉ antōṇiyik kāṇumuṉ nāṉ yātāyiṉum ākuka! anta arumporuḷik kaṇṭapiṉ, ataṉ kātaliṉ perukkiyum āḻattiyum uyarviyum aḷaviṭṭaṟinta piṉ, aḵtaṉṟi nāṉ vāḻtal kūṭumō? antōṇiyi otta matayāṉiyip piṇitta ikkātaṟ karaṅkaḷ iṉi sīsariyotta kuḷḷa nariyiyum piṇikka muṟpaṭumō? eṉṉa eṉ antōṇi maṉattuṭkoṇṭa karuttiṉ pōkku!” eṉṟāḷ.
atukēṭṭa antōṇi cari, appaṭiyāyiṉ uṉmuṭivu eṉṉa?’ eṉṟāṉ.
avaḷ, ‘’uṅkaḷ uṭaluṭaṉ eṉ uṭalic cērttu uṅkaḷ uyiruṭaṉ eṉ uyirum uṭaṉ varacceyvatē’ eṉṟāḷ..
avaḷ kātaliṉ uyarviyum, ataṉ muḻuvaliyiyum, ataṉ vīṟṟiyum aṉṟutāṉ avaḷ kaṇṭāṉ.
avaṉ mukam elliyillāta pēriṉpak katir vīci oḷirntatu. avvoḷi kaṇṭu kiḷiyōppātrāvum kātaṟ kaṭaliṉ kari kaṇṭavaḷāṉāḷ. avaḷ “iṉip piṟappu iṟappum iṉpatuṉpamum māṟi māṟi alipōl vantu vantu mōtum  ivvāḻkkik kaṭalik kaṇṭu nāṉ malivaṭiyēṉ. eṉ antōṇiyē ariya antōṇiyākak koṇṭu ataṉik kaṭappēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟi avaṉit taḻuvi muttamiṭṭāḷ.
vāḻkki muṟṟilum piṟar aṭiyum iṉpattiṉum avvoru noṭiyil avarkaḷ aṭinta iṉpam periteṉṉalām.
antōṇi kiḷiyōppātrāvi aṉṟu maṇanta maṇamakaḷiṉum uyarvāka eṭuttuṇittu muṭinīvi, “iṟivaṉ uṉṉik kākka! uṉṉāl piṟaviyiṉ payaṉ peṟṟuviṭṭa eṉakku iṉi eṉṉa kuṟi? uṉ kātal niṟivuṭaṉ māḷakkiṭṭiya immāḷvu vāḻviṉum niṟivuṭiyatu! iṉa nāṉ rōmaṉāka niṉṟu uyirviṭa vēṇṭum. nīyum ṭālamikaḷiṉ vaḻittōṉṟal eṉṟa takutikkum rōmaṉ kātali eṉṟa takutikkum iṇaṅka niṟivāḻveytuvāy! kātaṟ kaṭavuḷiṉ pātukāppukku uṉṉi viṭuttuc celkiṉṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟi avaḷi viṭṭakaṉṟu veḷiyiṭam ceṉṟu taṉ vāḷālēyē taṉṉi māyttuk koṇṭāṉ.
avaṉ iṟantatu kēṭṭa kiḷiyōppātrā uyiriḻanta uṭalpōl ceyalaṟṟu niṉṟāḷ. piṉ tēṟit taṉ kātaṟ kaṭaṉāṟṟa eṇṇumaḷavil sīsariṉ tūtaṉ vantu palavaki iṉiya moḻikaḷāl  avaḷi vayappaṭutta muyaṉṟāṉ. avaḷ avaṉi acaṭṭiyākap pārttut taṉ iṟuti viruppaṅkaḷi sīsarukku uṇarttu muṟiyil avaṉiṭam kūṟiṉāḷ:
“eṉakku sīsar rivātāyiṉ ataṉi eṉ putalvaṉiṭamum, eṉ kātalaṉ naṇpariṭamum kāṭṭuka! “
“nāṉ antōṇiyiṉ uyirē uyirāy avaṉ vāḻvē vāḻvāy vāḻntavaḷ; atupōl avaṉ māḷavē māḷvāy māḷat tuṇintu viṭṭēṉ. sīsar nāṭiya rōmap pēraraciyum, sīsar nāṭiya ekiptiyum iḻappatil eṉakku varuttamilli. antōṇi illā ulaki sīsar āḷaṭṭum. nāṉ antōṇi yiyum avaṉ vāḻum ulakiyumē āḷap pōkiṟēṉ.
“kātal taḷiyāl kaṭṭuṇṭirunta ciṅkattip piṉpaṟṟi nāṉ collukiṟēṉ. ciṟumippaṭṭa cūḻcci valiyāl ulakik kavarnta sīsar ivveṟṟulaki āḷaṭṭum.”
ivvāṟu perumitattuṭaṉ kūṟiviṭṭu avaḷ taṉṉiṭamē oru ciṟu peṭṭiyil karantuvittirunta koṭiya naccuppampu oṉṟiyeṭuttu ‘’ ‘itukāṟum piḷḷipōl uṉṉi vaḷarttēṉ. ippōtu naṉṟi maṟavātu eṉ kātaṟkaṉiyuṭaṉ eṉṉic cērppippāy’ eṉṟu kūṟi, ataṉ nañci uṭalil ēṟṟāḷ. ari noṭiyil antōṇiyiṉ uṭaluṭaṉ avaḷ uṭal kiṭappa uyir antōṇiyi nāṭic ceṉṟatu.
iṟutiyil akṭēviyas sīsar vantu kātalaracaṉum kātalaraciyumāy māḷvilum paṇiyātu vīṟuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiya attuṇivarik kaṇṭāṉ. oru noṭi avaṉ ulakiyal aṟivukūṭak kalaṅkiyatu. tamatu tannalattālum rōmiṉ cīrkulivālum itukāṟum iṟanta oppaṟṟa rōmat talivarkaḷi eṇṇip pārttāṉ. “vīrattukku iriyākiya jūliyas sīsareṉṉa, oḻukkattiṉ uyarkuṉṟākiya purūṭṭa seṉṉa, naṭpiṉ aṇikalamākiya kāsiyeṉṉa,  kaṭiciyil kātaliṉ oppaṟṟa cikaramākiya ivvīra antōṇiyeṉṉa, ipperuntaki rōma vīrar aṉivarum māṇṭaṉarē! rōmiṉ peruvāḻviṉ ūṟṟukaḷāṉa ivarkaḷ pōṉapiṉ pāliyāy viṭṭa ippuṉmi ulakō ippāviyēṉukkuk koṭuttu vittu!” eṉṟu maṉamāḻkik kaṇṇīrukuttāṉ.
ulakap pēraraci veṉṟa veṟṟivīraṉatu veṟṟiyic ciṟumip paṭuttiṉa kātaṟ pēraracaṉum pēraraciyum ākiya av iruvaruṭiya   uyartaṉik kuṇaṅkaḷi eṇṇi eṇṇi ekiptiyarum rōmarum āṟṟoṇāt tuyareytiṉar.
4. naṉku muṭivuṟiṉ nalamēyaṉittum
(All is well that ends well)
kati uṟuppiṉar
āṭavar:
1.  korārṭu: ariya maruttuvaṉ-heleṉā tanti.
2. perṭṭiram: kālañ ceṉṟa rūsilāṉ perumakaṉ putalvaṉ-heleṉāviṉ kātaluk kaḷāy avaḷi veṟuttum iṟutiyil maṇantu ēṟṟukkoṇṭāṉ.
3. pirāṉcu aracaṉ: kālañ ceṉṟa rūsilāṉ perumakaṉiṭam paṟṟu koṇṭavaṉ -heleṉāviyum perṭṭiramiyum maṇaviṉiyāl iṇittavaṉ.
peṇṭir:
1. rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭi: rūsilāṉ perumakaṉ maṉivi-perṭṭirām tāy-heleṉāviṉ kātalukkut tuṇi tantavaḷ.
2. heleṉā: maruttuvaṉ korārṭu makaḷ-tanti iṟantapiṉ, rūsilāṉ peruṅkuṭiyil paṇiyāḷāy perṭṭirāmik kātalittu aracaṉutaviyāl maṇantāḷ.
3. tayāṉā: piḷāreṉcu nakariluḷḷa naṅki-heleṉāviṉ ārūyirt tōḻiyāṉavaḷ-perṭṭiram takāk kātalukkāḷāy ataṉi maṟuttavaḷ.
4. mūtāṭṭi: tayāṉāviṉ tāy.
katic curukkam
korārṭu ariya maruttuvaṉ. avaṉ iṟantapiṉ avaṉ makaḷ heleṉā rūsilāṉ peruṅkuṭiyil paṇiyāḷāy amarntāḷ. pirāṉcu aracaṉ naṇpaṉākiya rūsilāṉ perumakaṉ iṟantapiṉ avaṉ makaṉ perṭṭirami aracaṉ amiccar mūlam aḻittut taṉṉiṭam vittuk koṇṭāṉ. avaṉiṭam uḷḷūṟak kātalkoṇṭa heleṉā, appakiyil rūsilāṉ  perumāṭṭiyiṉ tuṇipeṟṟu aracaṉiṭam ceṉṟu avaṉatu pāṇṭu nōyit tantiyiṉ arumaruntoṉṟāl kuṇap paṭuttiṉāḷ. aracaṉ  makiḻcciyuṟṟu avaḷukkut taṉ kaṇiyāḻiyik koṭuttatuṭaṉ avaḷ virumpiya āṭavaṉi maṇa muṭippatākac colla, perṭṭiramiyē virumpi maṇa muṭittāḷ. perṭṭiramiyē virumpi maṇa muṭittāḷ. poṭṭiram avaḷi veṟuttu rūsilāṉa viṭṭuviṭṭup piḷāreṉcu ceṉṟu, aṅkē paṭit talivaṉāyt tayāṉā eṉṟa peṇṇik kātalittāṉ.
ciṉṉāḷil maṉa veṟuppuṟṟa heleṉāvum veḷiyēkit tirintu, iṟutiyil taṟceyalāy atē piḷāreṉcil vantu tayāṉāviṉ ārūyirt tōḻiyāy avaḷ tuṇiyāl avaḷuṭiyil perṭṭiramuṭaṉ taṉayiṭamaṭintu, avaṉ kātalip peṟṟu, avaṉ kaṇiyāḻiyi vāṅkik koṇṭāḷ. itaṟkiṭiyil rūsilāṉukkuc ceṉṟu maṇa makkaḷ pirivu kēṭṭu varuntiya aracaṉ, perṭṭiramiyaḻittu heleṉā paṟṟi ucāvi, avaṉ avaḷi veṟuttu vēṟu peṇṇik kaḷavu muṟiyil maṇantatākak kūṟiṉāṉ. atēcamayam maṟiviliruntu heleṉāvum tayāṉāvum vantu kaṇiyāḻi kāṭṭip perṭṭiramiṉ maṉatti māṟṟiṉar. perṭṭiram heleṉāvuṭaṉ vāḻntāḷ. aracaṉ utaviyāl tayāṉā iṉṉoru perumakaṉi maṇantāḷ.
1.  kikkiḷik kātal
pirāṉcu nāṭṭil 1korārṭu eṉṟa ōr ariya maruttuvaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉ nōy ārāycciyilum maruntārāycciyilum oppaṟṟavaṉ. annāḷiya maruttuvaraṟivukku appāṟpaṭṭa pala putiya maruntu vakikaḷ avaṉukkut terintiruntaṉa.
korārṭukku 2heleṉā eṉṉum putalvi orutti iruntāḷ. korārṭu avaḷukku ettakiya poruṭkuviyum vikkavilli. āyiṉum avaṉ taṉ maruntu vakikaḷiyum avaṟṟi vaḻaṅkum muṟikaḷiyu maṭṭum avaḷukkuk kaṟpittiruntāṉ.
tanti iṟantapiṉ heleṉā taṉ tantikku aṟimukamāṉa 3rūsilāṉ eṉṟa peruṅkuṭiyiṟ ceṉṟu, paṇiyāhḷay amarntiruntāḷ. ap peruṅkuṭiyiṉ peruntalivaṉāyirunta paḻiya rūsilāṉ perumakaṉār iṟantuviṭṭamiyāl, iḷiñaṉākiya avar makaṉ 4perṭṭiram putiya rūsilāṉ perumakaṉ āṉāṉ.
paḻiya rūsilāṉ perumakaṉār pirāṉcu aracaṉukku neruṅkiya naṇpar. ākavē, aracaṉ avar makaṉākiya perṭṭiramik kāṇa virumpi avaṉi aḻittuvarum poruṭṭut taṉ amiccaṉāṉa 5lāpiyū eṉpavaṉi, avaṉpāl aṉuppiṉāṉ. perṭṭiram avvamiccaṉuṭaṉ puṟappaṭṭu aracaṉik kāṇacceṉṟāṉ.
rūsilāṉ kuṭiyil kālavaṭi vittu mutal heleṉātaṉ neñci muṟṟilum perṭṭiramukku paṟikoṭuttāḷ. āyiṉum, taṉ eḻimiyum tuṇiyaṟṟa taṉmiyum eṅkē, avaṉ uyarkuṭiyum pukaḻum eṅkē, eṉṟu avaḷ maṉam cōmpiṉāḷ.
perṭṭirāmiṉ tāyāṉa rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭi heleṉāviṉ maṉaniliyi uyttuṇarntu koṇṭāḷ. avaḷ pārvikku heleṉāviṉ ēḻimiyi viṭa avaḷ aḻakum kuṇamumē viḷakkamākat tōṉṟiṉa. ākavē avaḷ heleṉāviṉ kātalukku iṇakka maḷittāḷ. āyiṉum, perṭṭiram avaḷi matiyāmal acaṭṭiyāyiruntāṉ. ataṉāl avaḷ mikavum maṉaccōrvu aṭiya nērntatu.
perṭṭiram pōṉapōtu heleṉā, avaṉ pōṉa pakkamē nōkki niṉṟu, “antō neñcārak kalantu uṟavāṭāviṭiṉum ituvariyiṟ kaṇṇāravēṇuṅ kāṇa muṭintatu; iṉṟu ataṟkum vaḻiyilliyē” eṉṟu maṉa māḻkiṉāḷ.
perṭṭirami aḻittup pōka vanta amiccaṉ aracavik kāriyaṅkaḷ palavum pēcikkoṇṭiruntapōtu, aracaṉ pāṇṭu nōyiṉāl varuntukiṉṟāṉeṉṟum, ataṟku araṇmaṉi maruttuvar aṉivarum paṟpala maruntukaḷ koṭuttum kuṇam vantapāṭilli eṉṟum kūṟiyatu avaḷ niṉiviṟku vantatu. varavē, avaḷ maṉattil cila āḻnta eṇṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. “ā! eṉ tanti kuṟittut tantu pōṉa putuvaki maruntukaḷuḷ pāṇṭu nōykkāṉa maruntum oṉṟaṉṟō? ataṉ mūlam oruvēḷi eṉ eṇṇaṅkaḷ īṭēṟumāyiṉ evvaḷavu ciṟappākum” eṉṟa avaḷ vāyviṭṭuk kūṟiṉāḷ.
avaḷ kūṟiya moḻikaḷi, aṭuttu niṉṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭirunta rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭi, “aracaṉ piṇiyum, im maṅki piṇiyum oruṅkē nīṅkutal tiruvuḷamāyiṉ, aḵtu aṅṅaṉamē niṟivēṟuka” eṉṟu maṉattuṭkoṇṭu, avaḷi aracaṉpāl viṭuttaṉaḷ.
heleṉā, aracaṉi aṇukuvatē mutalil arumiyāka iruntatu. aṇukiyapōtum avaḷ marunti aracaṉ uṭkoḷḷuvataṟku avaṉ aviyōr oruppaṭavilli. inniliyil, aracaṉ kātali icceyti viḻuntatu. taṉatu nalviṉip payaṉāl tūṇṭappaṭṭu avaṉ, “eppaṭiyum nōy kuṇamaṭivatākak kāṇavilli. piṟa maruttuvar aṉivarum kiviṭṭaṉar. ipputu maruntālkuṇam ēṟpaṭāviṭṭālum kēṭeṉṉa? aḵtu oru vēḷi eṉ uyir koḷḷum nañcāy iruntāluṅkūṭa ivvēḷikku aḵtu eṉṉi nōyiṉiṉṟum tappa vikkum karuviyākavē amiyum” eṉṟu kūṟi avaḷi varaviṭuttāṉ. piṉpu avaḷ korārṭiṉ putalvi eṉṟum maruntu korāṭṭiṉ putu maruntukaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉṟum kēṭṭapōtu aracaṉukkup piṉṉum nampikki uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭiyum heleṉāvum kaṇṭa kaṉavu naṉavāyiṟṟu. ammaruntu uṭkoṇṭa iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷil aracaṉ nōyiṉ aṭiyāḷamē illātapaṭi muḻumiyum kuṇamaṭintāṉ. ākavē, avaṉ maṭṭaṟṟa makiḻcci koṇṭu avaḷukku vili matippaṟṟa oru kaṇiyāḻiyip paricāka vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. atōṭu avaviyil yāri vēṇṭumāṉālum avaḷ maṇantukoḷḷalām eṉṟum, avaḷukku avaṉ uṟuti koṭuttāṉ. heleṉā, aṅkē vēṟu yārmītum kaṇṇeṭuttup pārāmal rūsilāṉ peru makaṉākiya perṭṭiramiyē kaṇavaṉākat terinteṭuttāḷ.
perṭṭiram, paḻakka mikutiyiṉālum paṇiyāḷ eṉṟa puṟakkaṇippiṉālum heleṉāviṉ aḻakiyum, uyar kuṇaṅkaḷiyum matiyātavaṉāyiruntāṉ. ākavē, taṉ viruppattiṟku māṟāka avaḷ taṉ mītu cumattappaṭṭati avaṉ veṟuttāṉ. āyiṉum eṉ ceyvatu? aracaṉ āṇiyi maṟukka muṭiyātu; ātalāl, perṭṭiram avaḷi vēṇṭā veṟuppāka maṇantu koṇṭāṉ.
āyiṉum, maṇattittāṉ oruvar valintu cumatta muṭiyumēyaṉṟik kātalic cumatta muṭiyātaṉṟō? heleṉā, ‘kātalaṟṟa maṇam’ eṉṉum kaṭu narakiṟku āḷāṉāḷ. maṇa nāḷilēyē perṭṭiram, avaḷ pakkam pārāmal kaṭukaṭuttup pēcavum cīṟiviḻavun toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. avaṟṟi yellām avaḷ poṟumiyē uruvāka niṉṟu tāṅki vantatum, avaṉ avaḷ mēluḷḷa veṟuppic caṟṟum kuṟikkāmal avaḷit tiṭṭi, “eṉ kuṭipperumiyiyum poruḷiyum eṇṇittāṉē eṉ talikkuk kaṇṇivittāy. nī avaṟṟiyē pōy maṇantu vāḻ” eṉṟu avaḷi rūsilāṉukkē aṉuppiṉāṉ. aṉuppi viṭṭup palanāṭukaḷilum tirintu iṟutiyil piḷāreṉcu eṉṉum nakaram vantu, annakar aracaṉatu paṭiyiṟ cērntāṉ. vēṇṭā maṉiviyuṭaṉ vāḻvati viṭap pōril māḷvatē nallatu eṉṟa eṇṇattuṭaṉ, avaṉ vēṇṭumeṉṟē pōr muṉikaḷiṟ ceṉṟu niṉṟu kālaṉi virumpi vaḻipāṭu ceytāṉ. āṉāl, ivvakiyiṉāl avaṉukku, avaṉ virumpiya muṭivu ciṟitum kiṭṭavilli. nērmāṟāka avaṉ veṟṟiyum pukaḻum mikuntu paṭit talivaṉ āvataṟkē aḵtu utaviṟṟu. 
rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭi, mutalil, heleṉāvukku āṟutalāka, “perṭṭiram cīṟṟan taṇintu varuvāṉ; poṟuttiru,” eṉṟu kūṟivantāḷ. āṉāl, tiṅkaḷ oṉṟiraṇṭāyiṉa. varum vaki etuvuṅ kāṇōm. heleṉāvukku, rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭikkō, ulaka veṟuppup piṉṉum mikutiyāyiṟṟu.
inniliyil, perṭṭiramiṭamiruntu kaṭitamoṉṟu vantatu. vaṟṟaṟpāliyil nīr vēṭkiyāṟ ceyaliḻantavaṉ, nīreṉṟa peyarik kēṭṭavuṭaṉē uyirtteḻuvatu pōla, heleṉā uḷḷantuṭikka eḻuntu ceṉṟāḷ. rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭiyum ellām valla iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅkik kaṭitattip pirittup pārttāḷ. pārttavuṭaṉē avaḷ kikaḷum uṭalum tuṭikka, “cī ciṟumippaṭṭa patarē” eṉṟukūṟi nilattiṟ cāyntāḷ.
akkaṭitattil, “perṭṭiram maṇa nāḷiṟ koṇṭa veṟuppuc caṟṟum māṟātu. tāyē, heleṉāvukku uriyavaṉ nāṉallaṉ; avaḷ uṉṉiyum uṉ kuṭiyiyum poruḷiyum virumpiṉāḷēyaṉṟi, eṉṉiyō eṉ kātaliyō virumpiṉavaḷ allaḷ; avaṟṟiyē avaḷ aṭika; nāṉ iṉi avaḷukkum uṉakkum uriya avvīṭṭil nuḻiyēṉ,” eṉṟu eḻutiyiruntāṉ.
heleṉāviṉ uḷḷattil pālip putarpōl ularntu niṉṟa kikkiḷik kātaliyum ik kaṭitattaṉ tīmoḻikaḷ erittaṉa... “it tīyavaṉiṉ tīviṉikkāka, vīṭṭukkuriyavar vīṭṭit tuṟappatā? vēṇṭām; nāṉē pōyviṭukiṟēṉ,” eṉṟu moḻintu avaḷ veḷi- yēṟiṉāḷ. rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭi evvaḷavō taṭuttum, avaḷ puli kaṇṭu maruṇṭōṭupavaḷ pōl ōṭiṉāḷ.
2. mātar naṭpu
appoḻutu avaḷ uḷḷam paṭṭa pāṭṭi yārē kaṇittuk kūṟavallār? oru nēram, ‘kātalaṟṟa ivvulakiṉiṉṟu akaṉṟu viṭuvōmā’ eṉṟu eṇṇuvāḷ. āṉāl aṭutta nēram, ‘kātalaṉaṟṟa maṟu ulakil eṅṅaṉam ceṉṟu vāḻvēṉ’ eṉṟu eṇṇi alikkaḻivāḷ. 
avaḷ maṉatil oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākap perṭṭiramiṉ vāḻkkip paṭaṅkaḷ eḻunteḻuntu naṭaṉamiṭṭaṉa. ataṉ iṟutik kāṭciyi niṉikka niṉikka avaḷ aṅkaṅ kulintatu, perṭṭiram aṉṟu veṟuppu niṟinta kaṇkaḷōṭu avaḷi ēḷaṉa nakikoṇṭu nōkkip, “pētāy, eṉakkum uṉ kātalukkum ēṇi vittālum eṭṭātu. itō, eṉ kiyilirukkum ikkaṇiyāḻiyi eṉṉiṭamiruntu, ettakit tiṟamiruntālum uṉṉāl vāṅka muṭiyumā? atupōlattāṉ eṉ kātalip peṟum tiṟamum irukkum. nī ikkaṇiyāḻiyip peṟum nāḷē eṉ kātalip peṟum nāḷ” eṉṟu cīṟiyuritta kaṉal moḻikaḷ avaḷatu neñcattiriyil koḻuntu viṭṭerintu tuṉpamākiya pukiyi eḻuppiṉa.
eṉ ceyvāḷ pāvam! pañcu paṭappatikkum pātattiyuṭiya am melliyalāḷ, parukkik kaṟkaḷeṉṟum paṭarmuḷḷeṉṟum paṭukareṉṟum pārāmal alintu tirintu, iṟutiyil, viṉip payaṉiṉ valiyāl, perṭṭiram vāḻntuvanta atē piḷāreṉsil vantu cērntāḷ.
ā, vitiyiṉ iyipukiṭantavāṟu eṉṉē! heleṉāvi viṭṭoḻiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu veḷivanta perṭṭiramum, perṭṭiram vīṭṭilirātu akalavēṇṭu meṉṟu puṟappaṭṭa heleṉāvum ippaṭi ayal nāṭṭil vantoṉṟutal viyappuṭiyataṉṟō?
heleṉā avvūril oru mūtāṭṭiyuṭaṉ taṅki avaḷ ātaravil iruntu vantāḷ. ammūtāṭṭiyiṉ putalvi 6tayāṉā eṉpavaḷ heleṉāviṉ maṟupatippō eṉṟu collampaṭi otta paruvamum otta uruvamum cāyalum uṭiyavaḷāyiruntāḷ. ciṉṉāṭkaḷukkuḷ avviruvariṭiyē iṇipiriyā naṭpum paṟṟum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. appōtu avarkaḷ oruvarkkoruvar potuc ceytikaḷiyum, piṉpu tattam viruppu veṟuppukaḷiyum iṟutiyil tattam vāḻkkic ceytikaḷiyum kūṟik koṇṭaṉar.
avarkaḷ iruvarum, iru vēṟuṭalilum māṟip pukka orē uḷḷattiṉ iru pakutikaḷē eṉṉumpaṭi iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṉivuṭaṉ kalantu uṟavāṭi irukkum nāḷil, oru nāḷ heleṉā kalluṅ kariyum vaṇṇam taṉ kātaṟ katiyiyum kātalaṉatu puṟakkaṇippiṉ pōkkiyuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ. kēṭṭa tayāṉā attaṉiyum taṉakkē nikaḻntateṉat tuṭittut tattaḷittāḷ.
ellā vakikaḷilum tayāṉā heleṉāvi muṟṟilum ottu iruntāḷ. āṉāl, helaṉā, kātaloḷiyik kaṇṇuṟṟum ataṉ koṭiya vemmiyuṭ paṭṭuk karuki vāṭiya malar pōṉṟavaḷ. tayāṉāvō, kātaliṉ kaṭikkaṇippukkum puṟakkaṇippukkum oṉṟupōl āḷākāta naṟumalar pōṉṟavaḷ. heleṉāviṉ katiyik kēṭkak kēṭkat tayāṉāvukkut tuyar orupuṟam nēriṉum attakiya tuyariṉum, heleṉāvit taṉ vaḻippaṭutta aṭimiyākkum akkātal ettakiya teṉṟum attakiya kātali avaḷiṭam eḻuppiya talimakaṉ yāvaṉ eṉṟum aṟiyum vēṭki ataṉiṉum mikuntu niṉṟatu.
avvēṭki mikka tayāṉā, ataṟkēṟpat tāṉ avvakiyiṟ kaṇṭa orē nikaḻccayi heleṉāvukku eṭuttuk kūṟiṉāṉ. uyar niliyiluḷḷavaṉum aḻaku mikkavaṉum maṅyir karutti īrppavaṉumāṉa ōr iḷiñaṉ, tāṉ veṟuttut taḷḷiṉum pōkāmal taṉṉit tuṉpuṟuttu vatiyum avaṉiṭamō piṟarevariṭamō taṉakku heleṉāviṉ kātalip pōṉṟa uṇarcci eṟpaṭātatiyum avaḷ teḷinta uḷḷattōṭu eṭutturittāṉ.
palanāḷ iṅṅaṉam uriyāṭi varukiyil iruvarum, tammil oruvarip puṟakkaṇikkum kātalaṉiyum, maṟṟoruvar puṟakkaṇippukku āḷākuṅ kātalaṉiyum paṟṟip palavum pēcavar. appōtu heleṉā, avviruvarip paṟṟiya urikaḷum peritum ottirukka kaṇṭu viyantu orunāḷ tayāṉāviṭam avaḷi nāṭiya iḷiñaṉiṉ peyar eṉṉa eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ. avaḷ perṭṭiram eṉṟu kūṟiyatum, heleṉā tiṭukkiṭṭu, “eṉṉa, eṉṉa, perṭṭiramā? perṭṭiramā!” eṉṟāḷ.
heleṉāviṉ nilikaṇṭu viyappuṟṟa tayāṉā, “eṉ ārūyirt tōḻī, ēṉ, appeyaril eṉṉa? ataṉāl uṉakku ēṉ ittaṉi paraparappu?” eṉṟāḷ.
“tayāṉā! iṉṉum avarippaṟṟi caṟṟuk kūṟu. eṉ kātalaṉ peyarum atuvēyātalāl eṉ maṉam pataṟukiṉṟatu.”
“avar ētō rūsilāṉ kuṭiyic cērntavar eṉṟu eṉṉiṭam kūṟiṉār.”
ivvuri kēṭṭatum heleṉā iṭiyēṟuṇṭa aravameṉa alaṟi vīḻntu, “ā, koṭumi koṭumi; kaṭavuḷē ituvum uṉ tiruvuḷḷamō?” eṉṟu pulampi mayaṅkiṉāḷ.
ippōtu, “an naṅkiyar iruvar vāḻkkiyiyum iruvēṟu vakiyiṟ pāḻpaṭutti varupavar, iruvēṟu maṉitarallar; oruvarē,” eṉpatu avarkaḷukku viḷaṅkiṟṟu. atumutal avarkaḷ perṭṭiramippaṟṟi muṉpōl kaṉivuṭaṉ uriyāṭuvatilli. āṉāl, heleṉāmaṭṭum taṉṉi veṟutta perṭṭirami, avaṉ virumpaṉum tāṉ virumpātu āṭṭi vikkum ittōḻi mūlamē taṉ vayappaṭuttalām eṉṟu karuti, akkaruttit tayāṉāviṟkut terivittāḷ. tayāṉāviṉ tāy perṭṭiramiṭan taṉ makaḷukkuḷḷa veṟuppiyum, heleṉāviṉ tuyariyum kaṇṭu avarkaḷ ēṟpāṭṭiṟku iṇaṅkiṉāḷ.
3. paḻam naḻuvip pālil viḻuntatu
tayāṉā eṉṟum perṭṭirami veṟuttu avaṉ kātalip puṟakkaṇittē vantiruntāḷ. avaḷum avaḷ tāyum avaṉiṭam, “nīṅkaḷ periya iṭattic cērntavarkaḷ. uṅkaḷuṭaṉ otta maṇa vāḻkki koḷḷa eṅkaḷukkut takutiyilli,” eṉṟu teḷivākat taṅkarutti aṟivittu miruntaṉar. avvāṟiruntum, kātaliṉ tūymiyi muṟṟum aṟiyāta perṭṭiram, “maṇavāḻvu kiṭṭāviṭiṉuṅ kavaliyilli; evvakiyilum avaḷi aṭintē tīruvēṉ,” eṉṟu taṉatu ciṟṟiṉpa vēṭki tōṉṟak kūṟiṉāṉ.
kaṟpilum taṉ matippilum kuṟivuṟāc celvarkaḷākiya tayāṉāvum avaḷ tāyum ittīmoḻikaḷ kēṭṭu neruppiṟ paṭṭa puḻuveṉat tuṭittuppoṅkiṉum, heleṉāviṉ naṉmiyi eṇṇit tammi aṭakkikkoṇṭu avaṉ ēṟpāṭṭiṟku iṇaṅkiyavarkaḷ pōla naṭittu, avaṉit tayāṉā taṉiyē vantu kāṇuvataṟkāṉa iṭamum nēramum kuṟittaṉar. kuṟitta nērappaṭi kuṟitta iṭattil perṭṭiram vara, heleṉā tayāṉāviṉ tōṟṟattuṭaṉ aṅka vantu ārāk kātalōṭu avaṉuṭaṉ aḷavaḷāvi makiḻntāḷ. ammakiḻcci nērattil heleṉāviṉ kaṇiyāḻiyip perṭṭiram vāṅkik koṇṭāṉ. ataṟku māṟāka, avaṉ taṉa kaṇiyāḻiyi heleṉāviṭam koṭuttāṉ.
avvaṇṇamē aracaṉ rūsilāṉ vantu rūsilāṉ perumāṭṭiyiṉ peruntuyarkaṇṭu taṉṉili maṟantu kaṇṇīr ukuttuk kataṟiṉāṉ. piṉpu tiṟaṉuṭiya tūtariyum oṟṟariyum ēvi, heleṉāviyum,perṭṭiramiyum tēṭik koṇarumāṟu paṇittāṉ. avaṉ tūtarum oṟṟarum avarkaḷip pala nāḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷiṉum tēṭiṉar. iṟutiyil avarkaḷ, perṭṭiramip piḷāreṉcil kaṇṭu avvūr aracaṉ iṇakkam peṟṟu aṉik koṇṭu vantaṉar. icceyti aṟinta heleṉā, tāṉum tayāṉāviyum avaḷ tāyiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu nērē rūsilāṉ vantāḷ.
aracaṉ perṭṭiramiṭam heleṉāvip paṟṟi ucāviya pōtu, avaḷ uyiruṭaṉ irukkiṟāḷō māṇṭoḻintāḷō taṉakkoṉṟun teriyāteṉṟum, avaḷ ituvariyil māṇṭirāviṭṭāl virivil māḷavēṇṭumeṉṟē taṉ maṉam virumpukiṉṟateṉṟum, avaḷ māṇṭiruppiṉ, ataṉi aṟinta maṟunoṭiyē tāṉ iṉṉoru māti maṇañ ceytu koḷḷumṭi ēṟpāṭu ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉ teṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ. aracaṉ, “iṉṉoru māteṉṟatu yār?” eṉṟu kēṭkap perṭṭiram, tayāṉāvip paṟṟi moḻintu, avaḷuṭaṉ tāṉ ēṟkeṉavē uḷamuvantu kaḷaviyal muṟiyil maṇañ ceytu koṇṭatākavun terivittāṉ.
aracaṉ perṭṭiramiṭam elliyaṟṟa ciṉaṅ koṇṭā ṉāyiṉum, heloṉaviṉ ceytiyaṟiyātu etuvuṅ kūṟamuṭiyāta nilimiyiliruntāṉ.
appōtu, heleṉāvē muṉvaruvatu kaṇṭu, aracaṉ elliyaṟṟa makiḻcciyōṭu avaḷi etirkoṇṭu aḻittāṉ. uṭaṉē avaḷip perṭṭiramiṭam aḻittuc ceṉṟu avaḷi ēṟṟuk koḷḷumpaṭi kūṟap perṭṭiram, tāṉ maṉam kalantu nēcikkum maṅkiyiyaṉṟi, vēṟu yāriyum ēṟka muṭiyāteṉṟum, vēṇṭumāṉāl tāṉa virumpiya māti maṇakkum uṟutiyuṭaṉ ivaḷ iṟakkum varikkuṅ kāttirukkat tuṇintu viṭṭatākavum kūṟiṉāṉ.
aracaṉ ciṉaṅkoṇṭu, ‘iṉi ivaṉ iṟappatē naṉṟeṉṟu perṭṭirami vīḻttapukum elliyil avaṉ kiyiṟ ciṭanta kaṇiyāḻi aracaṉ kaṇkaḷukkup pulappaṭṭatu. “itu nāṉ heleṉāvukkuk koṭutta kaṇiyāḻi aṉṟō! iḵtu uṉ kiyil eppaṭi vantatu? kūṟuka” eṉṟu kēṭka, avaṉ tikittu miraḷa miraḷa viḻittāṉ.
annērattil heleṉā iṭiyiṭṭup perṭṭiramiṭam taṉakku oru vaḻakkirukkiṉṟa teṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. heleṉā eṉṉa collap pōkiṟāḷ eṉṟu perṭṭiram avaḷ pakkam tirumpiyatum avaḷ taṉkiyil aṇintirunta perṭṭiramiṉ kaṇiyāḻiyi avaṉiṭam kāṭṭi, “itaṉi nāṉ peṟukiṉṟa nāḷē tam kātalip peṟumnāḷ eṉṟu muṉpu moḻin tirukkiṉṟīr; avvāṟē, kaṇiyāḻiyip peṟṟēṉ; tam kātaliyum peṟṟavāḷāṉēṉ; ippōtuyātu kūṟukiṉṟīr” eṉṟu tīrnta kuralil taṉatu vaḻakki eṭutturittāḷ.
perṭṭiram atu kēṭṭu, nikaḻntatu iṉṉateṉṟaṟiya iyalāmal tiṭukkiṭṭu, “itu nāṉ eṉ kātalikkaṉṟō koṭuttatu! nī tiruṭiyō, allatu koli ceytōtāṉ itaṉik kavarntirukka vēṇṭum; appaṭiyāyiṉ, uṉṉi eḷitil viṭēṉ.” eṉṟāṉ.
heleṉā, “aracē, ēḻiyāyiṉum yāṉ tiruṭṭum, koliyum, koḷḷiyum aṟiyēṉ; iccelvar eṉ uḷḷam koḷḷi koṇṭavar. pōtāteṉṟu eṉ tōḻi oruttiyiṉ kātalit tiruṭṭāṟ peṟavum, avaḷ kaṟpik koḷḷi koḷḷavum muyaṉṟuḷḷār,” eṉṟu kūṟi, maṟintu niṉṟa tayāṉāviyum avaḷ tāyiyum veḷiyē varuvittu avarkaḷ mūlam kaṇavaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa kaḷaviyal maṇam avaṉ niṉittapaṭi vēṟoru mātuṭaṉaṉṟu; taṉṉuṭaṉēyē nikaḻntatu eṉpati viḷakkiṉāḷ.
perṭṭiram, heleṉāviṉ tiṟaṉiyum vaṭamīṉ pōl caṟṟum māṟāta niliyāṉa kātaliyuṅ kaṇṭu, tāṉ ceyta piḻikaḷukkellām avaḷpāl maṉṉippuk kēṭṭu avaḷi maṉamāra ēṟṟukkoṇṭāṉ. tayāṉāviṉiṭamum taṉ piḻi poṟukkumāṟu vēṇṭi, avaḷit taṉa taṅkiyākak karuti naṭattalāṉāṉ.
tayāṉāviṉ oppaṟṟa kaṟpuṟuti kaṇṭu viyanta maṉṉaṉ, heleṉāviṉ tiṟattukkup paricāka avaḷukkup perṭṭirami aḷittatu pōl, tayāṉāvukku taṉ perumakkaḷuḷ oruvarik kaṇavaṉākat tērnteṭuttu vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Goarard 
2.	Helena
3.	Rousillon 
4.	Bertram 
5.	Lafew  
6.	Diana

5. māṟāṭṭattāl nērnta pōrāṭṭam
Commedy of Errors
kati uṟuppiṉar
āṭavar:
1.ījiyaṉ: sirakkūs nakarc celvaṉ-iraṭṭiyarāṉa iru antipōlaskaḷiṉ tanti.
2. mūtta antipōlas: ījiyaṉ mūtta makaṉ-epīsas nakarap paṭittaliṉ-atiriyāṉā kaṇavaṉ.
3. mūtta turōmiyō: mūtta antipōlasiṉ paṇiyāḷ-atiriyāṉāviṉ paṇip peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉ.
4. iḷiya antipōlas: ījiyaṉ iḷiya makaṉ-tāyiyum tamiyaṉiyum tēṭa epīsas ntavaṉ.
5. iyi turōmiyō: iḷiya antipōlasiṉ paṇiyāḷ-piṉṉāl atiriyāṉā paṇip peṇṇiṉ taṅkiyi maṇantavaṉ.
6. epīsas nakarat talivaṉ:
7. meṉapōṉ kōmakaṉ: nakarattalivaṉ uṟaviṉaṉ-mūtta anti pōlasiṉ uyir naṇpaṉ-atiriyāṉāviṉ māmaṉ.
peṇṭir:
1. ījiyaṉ maṉivi: (māṟṟuruvil) episasil maṭat talivi-iru anti pōlaskaḷiṉ tāy.
2. atiriyāṉā: mūtta anti pōlasiṉ maṉivi-meṉapōṉ kōmakaṉ maruki.
3. atiriyāṉāviṉ taṅki: iṟutiyil iḷiya antipōlasiṉ maṉivi.
4. atiriyāṉāviṉ paṇippeṇ: mūtta turōmiyāviṉ maṉivi.
5. atiriyāṉāviṉ paṇippeṇṇiṉ taṅki: iṟutiyil iḷiya turōmiyōviṉ maṉivi.
6. naṭiki: mūtta antipōlasiṉ kātali.

katic curukkam
kappaluṭintataṉāl sirakkūs nakarac celvaṉ ījiyaṉuṭiya mūtta makaṉ antipōlasum, avaṉ vēliyāḷ turōmiyōvum epīsasil taṅkiṉar. antipōlas nakarat talivaṉ uṟaviṉaṉ meṉapōṉ naṭpiṉāl avaṉ marukiyākiya atiriyāṉāvi maṇantu paṭittalivaṉāy vāḻntāṉ. avaṉ paṇiyāḷ turōmiyōvum atiriyāṉāviṉ paṇip peṇṇi maṇantāṉ. ījiyaṉ maṉivi atē nakaril oru maṭattil cērntu maṭat taliviyāṉāḷ. ījiyaṉum mūtta anti pōlasuṭaṉ piṟantu avaṉiyē pōṉṟa uruvuṭiya iḷiya antipōlasum, turōmiyōviyē pōṉṟa uruvuṭiya avaṉ tampi iḷiya turōmiyōvum sirak kūsil vāḻntaṉar. tamiyaṉiyum tāyiyum tēṭi iḷiya anti pōlasum turōmiyōvum epīsas cella, avarkaḷip piṉpaṟṟic ceṉṟa ījiṉ sirakkūsarukketirāṉa epīsa! nakarattuc caṭṭap poṟiyuṭ paṭṭuk kōpat tīrppaḷikkap paṭṭāṉ. āyiṉum, avaṉ mītiraṅkit talivaṉ māli vari āyiram poṉ taṇṭam celuttit taṇṭaṉiyiliruntu tappumāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. itaṟkiṭiyil iru antipōlaskaḷum, iru turōmiyōkkaḷum orē uruvuṭiyavarāyiruntataṉāl, atiriyāṉā, avaḷ paṇippeṇ mutaliyōrum, mūtta antipōlasiṉiṭam kātal koṇṭa naṭikiyum avaḷ poṟ caṅkili ceyyak koṭuttirunta poṟkollaṉ oruvaṉum palavāṟu kuḻappamaṭintaṉar. iṟutiyil ījiyaṉ tūkkiliṭap pōkum taṟuvāyil avaṉ maṉiviyākiya maṭat taliviyum, piḷḷikaḷum oruṅkē vanta kuḻappamum taṟceyalāy nīṅka, aṉivarum makiḻntaṉar. iḷiya antipōlas atiriyāṉāviṉ taṅkiyiyum, iḷiya turōmiyō avaḷ paṉippeṇṇiṉ taṅkiyiyum maṇantaṉar.
1. kuṭumpappirivu
1sirakkūs eṉṟa nakaril 2ījiyaṉ eṉṟu oru celvaṉ iruntāṉ. toḻili oṭṭi, avaṉ cila kālam ayalnāṭu ceṉṟu vāḻavēṇṭiyiruntatu. appōtu avaṉukku, orē accil vārttavar pōṉṟa iraṇṭu putalvar piṟantaṉar. peyarilum vēṟṟumi illātapaṭi ījiyaṉ av viruvariyum 3antipōlas eṉṟē aḻittāṉ.
apputalvarkaḷ piṟanta arē nērattil annakar viṭutiyil ōr ēḻi mātiṉukkum iraṇṭu piḷḷikaḷ piṟantaṉar. avarkaḷum orē cāyalum tōṟṟamum uṭiyavarā yiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ tāy avarkaḷukkum 4turōmiyō eṉṟu orē peyariṭṭu aḻittāḷ. avaḷ mikavum ēḻi yāṉataṉāl avarkaḷit tāṉē vaḷarkka muṭiyāmal ījiyaṉiṭam oppaṭittāḷ. ījiyaṉum avviruvariyum taṉ piḷḷikaḷiṉ paṇiyāḷārāka vittu vaḷarttu vantāṉ.
iru antipōlaskaḷum iru turōmiyōkkaḷum ciṟuvarāyiruntapōtu peṟṟōr, tam tāy nāṭṭukkup pōkap payaṇamāyiṉar. āṉāl kālakkēṭṭiṉāl, vaḻiyil avarkaḷ ēṟicceṉṟa kappal puyaliṟpaṭṭu noṟuṅki viṭṭatu. kappalil iruntavaruḷ oru cilar maṭṭumē paṭakukaḷil tappiṉar.

ījiyaṉum avaṉ maṉiviyum tam piḷḷikaḷ ovvoruvariyum avaravar paṇippiḷḷikaḷuṭaṉē cērttu iraṇṭu marattuṇṭukaḷil kaṭṭi mitakka viṭṭārkaḷ. piṉ tāṅkaḷum taṉittaṉiyākap pāy marattil mitantu mitantu tappa muyaṉṟaṉar.
kāṟṟum aliyum aṭikkum vaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟu ellōrumē piḻittaṉar. āyiṉum oruvar piḻittati iṉṉoruvar aṟiya muṭiyavilli.
ūḻvaliyāl iṅṅaṉam citaṟiya akkuṭumpattiṉarip pala āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu atē ūḻvali 5epīsas eṉṉum nakaril vēṟu vēṟākak  koṇṭu vantu cērttatu. aṉivarukkum piṟar uyir piḻittatu teriyātatu pōlavē, aṉivarum ōr iṭattil vantu cērntatum avarkaḷukkut teriyāmalē iruntatu. ataṉ payaṉāka avarkaḷ palavakiyāṉa ikkaṭṭuk kaḷukkum kuḻappaṅkaḷukkum āḷāyiṉar.
avarkaḷ aṉivarilum, mutaṉ mutalāka epīsas vantavar ījiyaṉ maṉiviyum, mūtta antipōlasum avaṉ paṇiyāḷāṉa mūtta turōmiyōvumēyāvar. avarkaḷ annakarattuc cempaṭavar cilarāl kaṭaliliruntu kāppāṟṟap paṭṭaṉar. ciṟuvar iruvariyum tāyiṉiṭamiruntu pirittu accempavar piṟarkku aṭimikaḷāka viṟṟuviṭṭaṉar.
makkaḷi iḻanta tāy maṉamuṭintu, nakareṅkum pittuk koṇṭavaḷ pōl alintāḷ. appōtu tuṟavi oruvar avaḷukku meyyaṟivu pukaṭṭa,  avaḷ oruvāṟu maṉanteḷintuṉuru maṭattil iṟivaṉ paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭāḷ. avaḷ maṉat tūymiyik kaṇṭa maṭattut tuṟavimātar avaḷiyē ammaṭattiṉ taliviyākkiṉar.
ījiyaṉ makkaḷi vilikku vāṅkiyavaṉ, nakart talivaṉukku uṟaviṉaṉum peruvīraṉumākiya 6meṉēpōṉ kōmakaṉē. avaṉ mūtta antipōlasiṉ perumitat tōṟṟattik kaṇṭu avaṉip paṭiyil oru paṇiyāḷaṉākac cērkkumpaṭi nakart talivaṉit tūṇṭiṉāṉ.
paṭi vīrarāṉa antipōlas, taṉatu vīrattālum naṟkuṇattālum mikavum mēmpāṭeyti iṟutiyil, taṉakku utavi ceyta meṉepōṉ kōmakaṉ uyiriyum kāppāṟṟiṉāṉ. ataṉ payaṉāka nakarttalivaṉ avaṉiyē taṉ paṭit talivaṉāka amarttik koṇaṭāṉ. atumutal meṉēpōṉ kōmakaṉ avaṉippiṉṉum arumiyākap pārāṭṭit taṉ marukiyākiya 7atiriyāṉāvi avaṉukku maṇañ ceytu koṭuttāṉ.
antipōlasiṉ paṇiyāḷākiya turōmiyōvum atiriyāṉāviṉ paṇippeṇ oruttiyi virumpi maṇañ ceytukoṇṭu, avaḷuṭaṉēyē vāḻntu vantāṉ.
mūtta antipōlas iṅṅaṉam epīsasil nal vāḻvuṭiyavaṉāyiruppatu sirakkūs nakarilirukkum avaṉ tantiyākiya ījiyaṉukkum, iḷiya antipōlasukkum teriyātu. ākavē avarkaḷ avaṉip paṟṟik kavalikoṇṭu avaṉi eṉṟa kāṇpōmō eṉṟu taṉittaṉiyāka ēṅkiyiruntaṉar.
pala āṇṭukaḷ iṅṅaṉam kaḻintum aṇṇaṉip paṟṟiya ceyti etuvum teriyātatu kaṇṭu iḷiya antipōlas tantiyi aṇuki, “appā, iṉṉum nām vāḷā iruppatu naṉṟaṉṟu; nāṉāvatu pōy ammiyiyum aṇṇaṉiyum tēṭi varukiṟēṉ” eṉṟāṉ.
ījiyaṉukkum uṇmiyil maṉiviyip paṟṟiyum makaṉip paṟṟiyum kavali uṇṭu. āyiṉum, avarkaḷit tēṭat taṉ iḷiya makaṉi aṉuppiṉāl eṅkē ivaṉiyum iḻakka vēṇṭi vantuviṭumō eṉṟa añciṉāṉ. eṉiṉum, vēṟu vaḻiyiṉṟi avaṉ avarkaḷit tēṭumpaṭi iḷiñaṉi aṉuppat tuṇintāṉ.

iḷiya antipōlas iḷiya turōmiyōviyum, uṭaṉkoṇṭu pala iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu taṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāṉiyum nāyiyum tēṭiyalintu kaṭiciyāka epīsas nakaram vantu cērntāṉ. annakarukkum sirakkūsiṟkum neṭunāḷ pakimi uṇṭu eṉpati avaṉ aṟivāṉatalāl. tāṉum paṇiyāḷum vēṟṟu nakarattārāka urumāṟi uṭceṉṟaṉar. ceṉṟu eṅkuñ cuṟṟi ōyvuṟṟu, aṅkuḷḷa viṭuti oṉṟil taṅki iḷippāṟiṉar.
mutalil maṉiviyiyum oru piḷḷiyiyum iḻantataṉāl oru kaṇṇum kiyum iḻantavaṉ pōṉṟirunta ījiyaṉ, eñciya kaṇṇum kiyumāy aminta iḷiya makaṉiyum piriya nērntatiṉāl mikavum maṉamuṭintāṉ. āṉāl, eppaṭiyum avviḷiyōṉ muṉ iḻanta maṉiviyiyum makaṉiyum tēṭi aḻittuk koṇṭu varuvāṉ eṉṟa nampikkiyil ījiyaṉ cilakālam kaḻittāṉ. nāḷāka āka an nampikkiyum maṟiyalāyiṟṟu. iṟutiyil avaṉ poṟumi iḻantu, mutaliṟ kāṇāmaṟ pōṉavariyum piṉpu avarkaḷit tēṭic ceṉṟavariyum cērttut tāṉē ceṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭippateṉat tuṇintu puṟappaṭṭāṉ.
2. ījiyaṉ tuyar
iḷiya antipōlasi epīsas pakkam iḻuttuc ceṉṟa atē ūḻ, ījiyaṉiyum annakariṉ pakkamē koṇṭu vantu cērttatu.
epīsasukkum sirakkūsiṟkum iṭiyil pakimi uṇṭu eṉpati ījiyaṉ maṟantu sirakkūsar uṭiyilēyē nakaril pukuntu viṭṭāṉ. ākavē nakark kāvalar avaṉip piṭittukkoṇṭu pōy nakart talivaṉ muṉ niṟuttiṉar.
talivaṉ īraneñciṉaṉāyiṉum naṭunili piṟaḻātavaṉ. ātalāl ījiyiṉiṉ āṇṭu mutircciyik kaṇṭu parivuṟṟāṉāyiṉum, nakarc caṭṭattit tavirkkak kūṭāmal āyiram veḷḷi taṇṭa vari celutta vēṇṭum; aṉṟēl avaṉ taliyiḻattal vēṇṭumeṉat tīrppaḷittāṉ.
ījiyaṉ taṉ tuyaramikka varalāṟu muṟṟum nakart talivaṉukku eṭutturittāṉ. atukēṭṭu maṉamiraṅkiya attalivaṉ, taṇṭaṉiyi niṟivēṟṟa māli variyil tavaṇi koṭukkak kūṭumeṉṟum, ataṟkuḷ nakaril naṇpar evarēṉum iruntāṟ kaṇṭupiṭittu āyiram veḷḷiyik koṭuttu uyir piḻikkalāmeṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉ.
ījiyaṉ ittīrppuk kēṭṭu, aṉṟē taṉ uyir akaṉṟateṉṟu karutiṉāṉ. āyiṉum, mālivariyil nēramirukkiṉṟatu. orukāl ataṟkuḷ iṟiyaruḷ ētēṉum vaḻikāṭṭutaluṅ kūṭum eṉṟa nampikkiyuṭaṉ cuṟṟic cuṟṟi alintāṉ. māli aṇuka aṇuka acciṟu nampikkiyum vaṟaṇṭu pōyiṟṟu.
kaṟuttiruṇṭa mukiliṉiṭiyilum veḷḷiya miṉṉaṟ koṭi tōṉṟi oḷirvatupōla, nampikkiyaṟṟut tuyar cūḻnta iṭattilum maṟintu niṉṟaḷikkum iṟiyaruḷ oḷi vīcum eṉpati yārē aṟivar! ījiyaṉ tāṉ āḷaṟṟut tuṇiyaṟṟut tavippatāka niṉittu maṉamaḻuṅkiya atē nāḷil, aṇmiyilēyē avaṉ maṉiviyum avaṉ piḷḷikaḷum iruntaṉar eṉpatu avaṉukkut teriyātaṉṟō? atilum, iṟiyaruḷāl oru makaṉ niṟicelvamum uyarniliyum uṭiyavaṉāy arukiluḷḷa toru māḷikiyil vīṟṟiruntāṉ eṉpati eppaṭi avaṉ aṟiyakkūṭum?
itaṟkiṭiyil iṅṅaṉam, orē vaṭivuṭiya iraṇṭu antipōlaskaḷum iraṇṭu turōmiyōkkaḷum annakaril iruntatu avarkaḷiṭiyē palavakiyāṉa maruṭcikaḷiyum, kuḻappaṅkaḷiyum uṇṭu paṇṇiṟṟu.
3. kuḻappaṅkaḷ
iḷiya antipōlas taṉ paṇiyāḷiṭam paṇappiyik koṭuttuk, kaṭikkup pōyc camiyalukkāṉa poruḷkaḷ vāṅkivara aṉuppiviṭṭut tāṉ nakar pārkkum eṇṇattuṭaṉ terukkaḷil cuṟṟik koṇṭiruntāṉ.
mūtta antipōlas atē nērattil taṉatu paṭi niliyattil iruntāṉ. uṇavu nēramākiyum toḻil mikutiyāl aṉṟu avaṉ vīṭu cella muṭiyāmal kālantāḻtta nērntatu. avaṉ maṉiviyākiya atiriyāṉa akkālat tāḻvukkuk kāraṇan teriyāmal palavakiyil taṉmaṉatti araṭṭalāṉāḷ. 
avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉiṭam māṟāp paṟṟu vāyntavaḷāyiṉum, aiya maṉappāṉmiyāl alikkaḻiyum neñciṉaḷāyiruntāḷ. itaṉāl avaḷ avaṉukku nēram vēḷi eṉpatiṉṟi eppōtum tontaravu koṭuttu vantāḷ. aṉṟikku muntiṉa nāḷum avaḷ aṅṅaṉam tontaravu ceyta pōtu antipōlas, “ippaṭi nī eṉṉi eṉṟum arippatāyiṉ, nāṉ uṉṉi viṭṭuviṭṭu vēṟu maṉivitāṉ pārttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum” eṉṟu ciṉantu kūṟiṉāṉ.
veḷḷiyaṟiviṉaḷākiya atiriyāṉā, avaṉ mēlīṭākak kūṟiya icciṉa moḻikaḷi appaṭiyē meyyeṉak koṇṭu maṉamuḷintāḷ. iṉṟu, ataṟkēṟpa, antipōlas vēḷiyil vīṭṭukku varak kālan tāḻttatu kaṇṭu, avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉ taṉṉiṭam coṉṉapaṭiyētāṉ taṉṉi viṭṭu viṭṭāṉō eṉṟu mikka accaṅkoṇṭāḷ. eṉavē, avaḷ taṉ paṇiyāḷākiya mūtta turōmiyōvi aḻittut taṉ kaṇavaṉit tēṭikkoṇṭu varumpaṭi aṉuppiṉāḷ.
turōmiyō taṉ talivaṉit tēṭikkoṇṭu pōkumpōtu, vaḻiyil iḷiya antipārasik kaṇṭāṉ. kaṇṭu avaṉ taṉ talivaṉē eṉṟu eṇṇiya turōmiyō, talivi aḻikkiṉṟāḷ eṉṟu kūṟi vīṭṭukku iḻuttāṉ. āṉāl, avvanti polas, itturōmiyōvit taṉ paṇiyāḷākiya turōmiyōvē eṉak karuti vekuḷiyuṭaṉ, “eṉṉiṭam ēṉ nakiyāṭukiṟāy? eṉakkut taliviyumilli, koliviyumilli; ēṉ tontaravu ceykiṟāy?” eṉṟu cīṟiṉāṉ. turōmiyō pōkāmal piṉṉum pacuppuvatu kaṇṭu, avaṉi antipōlas niyappuṭit taṉuppiṉāṉ.
turōmiyō aḻutukoṇṭu taliviyiṉiṭam vantu, “ammaṇi, taṅkaḷ kaṇavar, nāṉ pōy aḻittatum kaṭuñciṉaṅ koṇṭu eṉṉi aṭittut turattiviṭṭār. mēlum, avar ‘eṉakkut taliviyumilli, koliyumilli’ eṉṟu veṟutturikkiṟār” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. atik kēṭṭatum, atiriyāṉāviṉ maṉakkuḻappam muṉṉiyiṉum paṉmaṭaṅku mikutiyāyiṟṟu. uṇarccic cuḻaliṟpaṭṭa avaḷ, maṉam patittut taṉ kaṇavaṉic cīṟṟan taṇittu aḻittu varat tāṉē puṟappaṭṭāḷ.
turōmiyō kūṟiya kuṟippip piṉpaṟṟi atiriyāṉā oru paṇip peṇṇuṭaṉ iḷiya antipōlas irukkum viṭutikku vantu cērntāḷ. turōmiyōvip pōlavē avaḷum avaṉit taṉ kaṇavaṉ antipōlasē eṉṟa niṉittāḷ. ākavē, avaḷ avaṉit taṉṉuṭaṉ vīṭṭukku vantu uṇavaruntumpaṭi vēṇṭiṉāḷ. antipōlas uṇmiyil avaḷ yār eṉṟē aṟiyātavaṉātaliṉ, oṉṟum viḷaṅkātu viḻittāṉ. caṟṟu muṉṉatākap paṇiyāḷ kūṟiya moḻikaḷik kūṭa, avaṉ, ‘vēṇṭumeṉṟē ēḷaṉamākak kūṟiya puṉicuruṭṭu’ eṉṟu karutiṉāṉ. ippōtu avaṉ katikku ēṟṟapaṭi tāḷamiṭa, ippeṇ eṅkiruntu vantāḷ eṉṟu avaṉ niṉittu viyappaṭintāṉ.
āṉāl, avaṉ evvaḷavukkevvaḷavu atiriyāṉāvi ṉiṭam ētilaṉāka otuṅki naṭantu koṇṭāṉō, avvaḷavuk kavvaḷavu avaḷ avaṉiṭam kaṇavaṉ eṉṟu urimi koṇṭāṭik keñci vaṟpuṟutti aḻikkalāṉāḷ. am moḻikaḷi maṟukka muṭiyāmiyiṉālum, paci vēḷiyā yiruntataṉālum, ippittalāṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ muṭivutāṉ eṉṉa eṉṟu terintukoḷḷum viruppattiṉālum avaṉ avaḷip piṉpaṟṟi avaḷ illatti aṭintāṉ.
atiriyāṉāvukkut taṅki orutti iruntāḷ. avaḷum atiriyāṉāip pōlavē aḻakumikkavaḷ. āṉāl avaḷiṭam atiriyāṉāviṉ paṭapaṭappō aiyamaṉappāṉmiyō ciṟitum illi. atiriyāṉā antipōlasi vaṟpuṟutti uṇavaḷikkum pōtellām, avaḷ caṟṟē akala niṉṟiruntāḷ. atiriyāṉāviṉ kaṭumoḻikaḷiviṭa, avaḷuṭiya kalaṅkiya iru viḻikaḷum, avvappōtu kūṟum aṉpu niṟinta arumoḻikaḷum antipōlasiṉ maṉatti mikutiyāka iyakkiṉa. avaṉ taṉvayamaṟṟavaṉāy avarkaḷuṭaṉ iṉṉuriyāṭi aṉpōṭu uṇṇalāṉāṉ.
4. kaṇavaṉ ēmāṟṟam
itaṉiṭiyil atiriyāṉāviṉ kaṇavaṉāṉa maṟṟiya antipōlas, maṟṟa nāḷiṉum mikutiyāka nērañceṉṟu vīṭṭiṟkut tirumpiṉāṉ. varumvaḻiyil taṉṉit tēṭivarum paṇiyāḷ turōmiyōvik kaṇṭu avaṉiyum aḻittuk koṇṭu vantāṉ.  āṉāl iruvarum vīṭu vantu cērntapoḻutu vīṭṭiṉ vāyil aṭittiruntatu. vīṭṭiṉuḷḷiruntu, atiriyāṉāvum avaḷ taṅkiyum yāruṭaṉō aḷavaḷāvip pēcuṅkural kēṭṭatu. veḷiyil katavit taṭṭiyatu avarkaḷukkuk kēṭkavilli. 
āṉāl, katavarukil irunta paṇippeṇkaḷ cilar, ‘yār aṅkē’ eṉṟu kēṭṭārkaḷ iruvarum tām iṉṉār eṉpatit terivitta aḷavil avarkaḷuḷ orutti kolleṉa nakittu, “ēṉiyā, ippaṭi aṭāppoy aḷakkiṟīr! talivarum eṉ kaṇavarākiya avar paṇiyāḷum uḷḷiruntu uṇṭu koṇṭirukka, avarkaḷ peyarāl eṉṉi ēmāṟṟukiṟīrē’ eṉṟu eṭutteṟintu kūṟi ēḷaṉañ ceytāḷ. atukēṭṭu mūtta antipōlas, kaṭuñ ciṉaṅkoṇṭu taṉ niliyattiyē nōkki mīṇṭāṉ.
avaṉ pōkum vaḻiyil oru naṭikiyiṉ vīṭu iruntatu. avaṉ cila nēraṅkaḷil poḻutu pōkkiṟkāka aṅkē ceṉṟu avaḷuṭaṉ pēciyiruppatuṇṭu. iṉṟu taṉ maṉivi taṉṉi avamatittati eṇṇi maṉam veṟuppuṟṟu, avaḷiṭam ceṉṟu caṟṟup pēcik kaḷittirukkalām eṉṟu karutiṉāṉ. avaḷum avaṉ varaviṉ arumiyaṟintu pōṟṟi, avaṉiṭam parivāy uriyāṭiṉāḷ. atukaṇṭu kaḷippuṟṟa antipōlas, tāṉ taṉ maṉikkeṉṟu ceytuvarum poṟcaṅkili oṉṟi avaḷukkut taruvatāka vākkaḷittāṉ. avaḷum avaṉ parivukku makiḻntu, taṉ kik kaṇiyāḻiyi eṭuttu avaṉ kiyil aṇivittāḷ.
poṟcaṅkiliyic ceyya ēṟṟukkoṇṭa poṟ kollaṉ. atu muṭintatum antipōlasik kāṇa vantu koṇṭiruntāṉ. vaḻiyil, avaṉ iḷiya antipōlasik kaṇṭu, avaṉē mūtta antipōlas eṉṟu niṉittu avaṉiṭam caṅkiliyik koṭuttāṉ antipōlas atu taṉataṉṟu eṉṟa pōtu, avaṉ ciṉaṅkoṇṭu “ippōtu eṉataṉṟu eṉpataṉāl eṉṉa payaṉ? umakkeṉac ceytāyiṟṟu; iṉi nīr ati ēṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatē” eṉṟu ati  avaṉiṭam eṟintu viṭṭup pōṉāṉ.
koñcanēraṅ kaḻittu appoṟkollaṉ oru kaṭaṉ kāraṉāl ciṟi piṭikkappaṭṭāṉ. kāvaluṭaṉ ciṟikkūṭattiṟkuc cellum vaḻiyil, avaṉ mūtta antipōlasik kaṇṭāṉ. kaṇṭavuṭaṉ caṅkilikkup paṇam koṭukkumpaṭi avaṉik kēṭka, antipōlas, “paṇam irukkaṭṭum, caṅkili eṅkē?” eṉṟāṉ. poṟkollaṉ, “ummiṭantāṉ caṟṟumuṉ koṭuttēṉē” eṉṟu tuṇivākak kūṟiṉāṉ. iruvarum vātāṭiṉār kāvalar, iruvariyum ciṟippiṭittu nakarttalivaṉiṭam aḻittēkiṉar.
iṅṅaṉam ciṟi cellutal, taṉ uyarnilikkut takāteṉa antipōlas eṇṇiṉāṉ. ākavē tāṉ vaḻiyil kaṇṭa iḷiya turōmiyōvit taṉ paṇiyāḷ eṉṟu niṉittut taṉ maṉiviyiṭam ceṉṟu tāṉ viṭutali peṟuvataṟkāṉa poruḷi uṭaṉē vāṅkivarap paṇittāṉ.
turōmiyō annērattil uṇmiyil taṉ talivaṉākiya iḷiya antipōlasit tēṭikkoṇṭu vantāṉ. ēṉeṉṟāl, avarkaḷ itaṟkumuṉ, ‘pittar pōṉṟu naṭakkum makkaḷiyuṭiya immāya nakari viṭṭu uṭaṉē nīṅkavēṇṭum’ eṉṟu uṟuti ceytiruntaṉar. ātalāl, iḷiya antipōlas, kappal puṟappaṭum nēratti aṟintu varumpaṭi taṉ turōmiyōvi veḷiyē aṉuppiyiruntāṉ. kappal oṉṟu aṉṟē puṟappaṭa iruntati aṟinta turōmiyō. ati aṟivikkattāṉ taṉ talivaṉ antipōlasi nāṭi vantāṉ. āṉāl taṉ ēṟpāṭukaḷukku ovvāta moḻikaḷi avaṉ ippōtu pakarntatu kēṭṭu turōmiyō, perituṅ kuḻappamuṟṟāṉ.
āyiṉum, ‘paṇiyāḷukkuriya kaṭaṉ kāriya kāraṇa ārāycciyaṉṟu; kaṭṭaḷippaṭi naṭappatēyām’ eṉat tuṇintu, avvantipōlasik kaṇavaṉ eṉṟu urimi pārāṭṭiya atiriyāṉāviṉ vīṭu ceṉṟu nilimiyit terivittup paṇam kēṭṭāṉ. kaṇavaṉiṭam elliyaṟṟa paṟṟu vittirunta atiriyāṉā, uṭaṉē patittuk koṇṭu tāṉē paṇattik koṭuttaṉuppiṉāḷ.
turōmiyō appaṇattik koṇṭu celkiyil iḷiya antipōlasik kāṇavē, avaṉ ‘nam talivaṉ itaṟkumuṉ kāvaliliruntāṉē; eppaṭi veḷivantāṉ’ eṉṟa viyappuṭaṉ paṇatti avaṉmuṉ vittāṉ. paṇam ētu eṉṟu antipōlas kēṭka, avaṉ, “nīṅkaḷ kēṭṭuviṭṭapaṭiyē uṅkaḷik kaṇavar eṉṟaḻitta ammātu tāṉ koṭuttāḷ” eṉṟāṉ.
caṟṟumuṉ oru poṟkollaṉ oru caṅkiliyik koṭuttuc ceṉṟatum, taṉ paṇiyāḷē muṉpiṉ muraṇāka naṭappatum kāṇa, iḷiya antipōlasiṉ mukattil ‘annakar ētō cūṉiyakkāriyiṉ mantira valiyiṟpaṭṭa māyanakar pōlum’ eṉṟa aiyam valuttatu.
inniliyil mūtta antipōlasuṭaṉ naṭpup pūṇṭa naṭiki aṅku vantu, taṉakkuc ceyta uṟutippaṭi caṅkili varātataṉāl taṉkaṇiyāḻiyit tiruppik koṭuttuviṭum paṭi avaṉi vēṇṭiṉāḷ.
iḷiya antipōlas, “nī yār eṉpatiyē nāṉ aṟiyēṉ, āyiṉum, ētō caṅkili eṉṟu nī kūṟuvataṟkēṟpa oruvaṉ iccaṅkiliyi eṉṉiṭam eṟintu pōṉāṉ” eṉṟu colli ataṉi avaḷiṭam koṭuttāṉ.
avaḷ caṅkiliyi eṭuttukkoṇṭu vīṭṭukkup pōkumvaḻiyil atiriyāṉāvik kaṇṭu, tāṉ aṟinta varalāṟukaḷellām colli, antipōlasukkuk kaṭṭāyam pittuttāṉ piṭittirukka vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu taṉ karutti veḷiyiṭṭāḷ.
5. āṭavar nalivu
atiriyāṉāvum taṉṉiṭam avaṉ noṭikku oruvakiyāka naṭappati ōrntu avaḷ kūṟuvatu uṇmiyāka irukkakkūṭum eṉṟu nampiṉāḷ. appōtu iḷiya antipōlasum avaṉuṭaṉ pūcaliṭṭa vaṇṇamāka iḷiya turōmiyōvum vantaṉar. tam kaṇavar pittuk koṇṭaṉar eṉṟeṇṇiya atiriyāṉāvum avaḷ paṇippeṇṇum avarkaḷiṭam naṭikiyiṉ ceytiyik kūṟi, avarkaḷi uṇmiyilēyē veḷikoḷḷac ceytaṉar. eppaṭiyāvatu tappiṉāl pōtum eṉṟu avarkaḷ timiṟik koṇṭōṭap  pārkkavē, peṇṭir avarkaḷ pittarātalāl aṭittu vippatu naṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭu avarkaḷip piṭikkac ceṉṟaṉar. āṭavar iruvarum ciṉantu eḻuntaṉarāyiṉum attakiya nerukkaṭiyil vēṟu vakiyaṟiyāmal ōṭicceṉṟu pakkattilirunta oru periya kaṭṭiṭattiṉuḷ nuḻintaṉar. atiriyāṉāvum paṇippeṇṇum aṅkum nuḻiya muyaṉṟaṉar.
akkaṭṭiṭamē ījiyaṉ maṉivi maṭattaliviyāka amarntu naṭattiya maṭamēyākum. iḷiya antipōlasum iḷiya turōmiyōvum atiriyāṉāvil turattappaṭṭut tañcam, tañcam eṉat taṉ maṭattiṟ pukuntapōtu, avaḷ, avarkaḷ taṉ piḷḷiyum paṇiyāḷumēyāvar eṉpati aṟiyātavaḷāyt taṉ iyaṟki irakka maṉappāṉmiyiṉāl avarkaḷukkut tuṇi tara muṉvantāḷ.
atiriyāṉavum avaḷ paṇippeṇṇum tam kaṇavar pittuk koṇṭavarātalāl, avarit tammiṭamē oppaṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉar. āṉāl, maṭattaliviyāṉa ījiyaṉ  maṉivi, “avarkaḷ aṟiviḻintu pittuk koṇṭāl, nīṅkaḷ amiti iḻantu pittuk koṇṭirukkiṟīrkaḷ; avarkaḷi ātarikkum nili uṅkaḷukkilli. nāṉē avarkaḷi ātarittu uṅkaḷiṭam oppuvikkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭāḷ.
mēlum, atiriyāṉā taṉ kaṇavaṉiṭam maṭṭaṟṟa paṟṟuk koṇṭavaḷāyiṉum, ataṉ kāraṇamākap poṟumiyum nalluṇarvum iḻantu, avaṉ mītu aiyaṅkoṇṭu avaṉukku ōyāta tontaravu koṭuttu varuvatiyum maṭattalivi uyttuṇarntu koṇṭāḷ. ākavē, avaḷ taṉatu iṉiya nēriya moḻikaḷāl atiriyāṉavukku avaḷ kuṟṟam teṉpaṭum vaṇṇam aṟivuri nalkiṉāḷ. atiriyāṉavum teruṭciyuṟṟup poṟumiyuṭaṉ kaṇavaṉ kuṟippaṟintu naṭappatāka ottuk koṇṭāḷ. āyiṉum, maṭattalivi avaḷi muṟṟum nampi antipōlasiyum turōmiyōviyum avaḷiṭam oppaṭikka maṉamillāmal avarkaḷ aṉaviriyum nakarttalivaṉiṭam koṇṭu pōy niṟutti avaṉ mūlam āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutiṉāḷ.
6. muṭivu
antipōlasiṉ tāy maṭattaliviyāy irunta immaṭattiṉ muṉpuṟamē annakariṉ tūkkumēṭi amintiruntatu. māli variyil naṇpar evariyuṅ kāṇāmiyāl taṇṭavari celutta vakiyillāta ījiyaṉik kāvalar kolit taṇṭaṉikkāka ammēṭikkuḷ koṇṭu vantaṉar. avaṉuṭaṉ, attaṇṭaṉiyi vēṇṭā veṟuppāka niṟivēṟṟum poṟuppi ēṟṟu nakarat talivaṉum vantāṉ.
itē nērattil, ciṟiyil vikkappaṭṭa mūtta antipōlas taṉ uyar niliyiṉ utaviyāl ciṟik kāvalaṉi accuṟutti vilakki viṭṭut turōmiyōvuṭaṉ attalivaṉ muṉ vantu, tamakku nakaril nērnta palavaki iṉṉalkaḷiyuṅ kūṟi, ‘iviyaṉittum yārō etirikaḷiṉ mayāmāyirukka vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu maṉṟāṭiṉāṉ. talivaṉ, aḵtu iṉṉateṉṟu aṟiyātu kuḻappamuṟṟāḷ.
ūḻvaliyāl ittaṟuvāyil, maṭattaliviyāka ījiyaṉ maṉivi, iḷiya antipōlasiyum iḷiya turōmiyōviyum atiriyāṉāvuṭaṉum paṇippeṇṇuṭaṉum kūṭṭik koṇṭu vantu taṉ maṭattiṉ muṉ naṭantavi aṉittum pukaṉṟāḷ. itaṟkuḷ, atiriyāṉāvum avaḷ paṇippeṇṇum tattam kaṇavar uruvuṭaṉ oruvarukkiruvar niṟpatu kaṇṭu elliyillā viyappum viḻippum aṭintaṉar. nakart talivaṉum avarkaḷik kaṇṭu, “iḵteṉṉa, ivarkaḷ vaṭivum, ataṉ niḻalum pōla ottu niṟkiṉṟaṉarē; ivarkaḷ iruvarō oruvarō” eṉa malivuṟṟāṉ.
appōtu tirumaṭat taliviyiṉ uḷḷattil oru pēroḷi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. avaḷ iru antipōlaskaḷiyum vāri eṭuttu, “ā, nīṅkaḷ eṉ piḷḷikaḷ allirō” eṉṟu aḻutāḷ. ījiyaṉum taṉ tuyaramellām maṟantu tāṉē piḷḷikaḷiṉ tanti eṉak kūṟik koṇṭu ōṭivantu, arukiruntu iṉpak kaṇṇīr ukuttāṉ.
maṭattalivi, iru antipōlaskaḷum orē uruvuṭiya tam iru piḷḷikaḷ eṉṟum. uṭaṉ irukkum oru turōmiyōkkaḷum aṅṅaṉamē paṇimātu orutti peṟṟa iraṭṭip piḷḷikaḷ āvar eṉṟum kūṟi, ījiyaṉ taṉṉi viṭṭu neṭunāṭ pirintirunta taṉ kaṇavaṉē eṉṟum, tām pirintukūṭiya ivviyattaku varalāṟu muṟṟum eṭutturittāḷ.
atukēṭṭu aṉivarum elliyilā viyappum makiḻcciyum eytiṉar.
iḷiya antipōlas atiriyāṉāviṉ taṅkiyiyum, iḷiya turōmiyō avaḷ paṇippeṇṇiṉ taṅkiyiyum virumpi maṇantaṉar.
nakart talivaṉ iḷiya antipōlasit taṉ karuvūlat talivaṉ ākkiṉāṉ. aṉivarum iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntu vantaṉar.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Syracuse
2.	Aegeon
3.	Antipolis
4.	Dromio 
5.	Ephesus
6.	Duke of Menophon  
7.	Adriana
eee




cēkcupiyar katikaḷ - 4
mutaṟ patippu - 1945
innūl 2002 il ēḻumali patippakam, ceṉṉi - 88. veḷiyiṭṭa patippi mūlamākak koṇṭu veḷivarukiṟatu.
1 - nāṉkām heṉri
(ḻanacēla aiē)
kati uṟuppiṉar
āṭavar:
1.nāṉkām heṉṟi aracaṉ: riccarṭukkup piṉ avaṉi vilakki aracaṉāṉavaṉ. riccarṭiṉ ciṟṟappaṉāṉa jāṉ āpkāṇṭiṉ makaṉ.
2.heṉri iḷavaracaṉ allatu hāl : nāṉkām heṉri aracaṉuṭiya mūttamakaṉ. iḷamiyil ḵpālsṭāḵp kūṭṭattāruṭaṉ iṉpa vāḻvilīṭupaṭṭavaṉ. 
3. jāṉ iḷavaracaṉ : heṉri iḷavaracaṉ tampi; nāṉkām heṉriyiṉ iḷiya makaṉ.
4. ōveṉ kiḷeṉṭōvar : vēls talivaṉ.
5.eṭmaṉṭ mārṭṭimar:  mārc iḷaṅkōvaṉ - riccarṭukkuc ciṟṟappaṉ makaṉ nāṉkām heṉriyiṉ periyappaṉ makaṉ-riccarṭiṟkup piṉ varaṉmuṟippaṭi aracukku urimiyuṭiyavaṉ- vēls talivaṉ ōveṉ kiḷeṉṭōvar makaḷi maṇantavaṉ.
6.nārtamparlantuk kōmakaṉ : riccarṭu aracaṉukketirāka nāṉkām heṉṟikku utavi ceytu, avaṉukkum etirākat tirumpiyavaṉ - heṉripersi allatu hāṭspariṉ tanti.
7. heṉri persi allatu hāṭspar : peruvīraṉ, aṭaṅkāt taṟutali - skāṭlantiliruntu paṭittalivaṉ ṭakḷasic ciṟip piṭittu viṭutali ceytu naṭpākkik kiḷarcciyil cērttuk koṇṭavaṉ. heṉri iḷavaracaṉuṭaṉ otta akaviyuṭiyavaṉ- avaṉ etiri.
8.ṭakḷas : skāṭlantup paṭittalivaṉ.
9.sarjāṉ ḵpālsṭāḵp: vīḻcciyaṟṟa peruṅkuṭiyāḷaṉ-oppaṟṟa nakiccuviyuṭiyavaṉ-iḷavaracaṉ kīḻnilit tōḻaruṭ ciṟantavaṉ.
10.kārlil talimakaṉ : riccarṭṭiṉ teyvīka urimiyi vaṟpuṟutti nāṉkām heṉriyāl koliyuṇṭavaṉ.
11.pārṭālp : sarjāṉ ḵpālsṭāḵpiṉ tōḻaruḷ oruvaṉ.
12.sar vālṭṭar piḷaṉṭ: aracar pakkattu niṉṟu ṣrūsparīc caṇṭiyiṉ muṉtūtaṉākac ceṉṟavaṉ.
13. vorsṭar: kiḷarccikkārar pakkattilirunta srūs pariccaṇṭiyiṉ muṉ tūtarākac ceṉṟavarkaḷ.
14. veṭarṉaṉ:
15. pāyiṉs : sar jāṉ ḵpāslṭāḵp tōḻaṉ.
16.sarjāṉ : pālsṭāḵpiṉ vēṟu tōḻaṉ.
17.taṇṭattalivaṇaṉ.
18.yārk perun talimakaṉ : iraṇṭām kiḷarcciyit talivaruḷ oruvaṉ.
19. mēprē : iraṇṭām kiḷarccit talivaruḷ oruvaṉ.
20. tiruvāḷar  poḷḷal : sar jāṉ ḵpālsṭāḵp cērtta cottippaṭi vīrarkaḷ.
21. tiruvāḷar vāymūṭi:
22. hēsṭiṅs : iraṇṭām kiḷarccit talivaruḷ oruvaṉ
23. vesṭu mōrlantu : iraṇṭām kiḷarccik kālattil jāṉ iḷavaracaṉuṭaṉ irunta talivaṉ.
24. vārvik perumakaṉ: aracaviyiluḷḷa perumakkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ.
25.sar heṉri kāskāyiṉ: sar jāṉ ḵpālsṭāḵpi veṟutta vaḻakku maṉṟat talivaṉ.
katiccurukkam
nāṉkām heṉri aracaṉ taṉakku muṉ irunta riccarṭu aracaṉi vīḻtti aracaṉāṉavaṉ. riccarṭu aracaṉiṭam poṟāmi koṇṭa perumakkaḷ ātaraviṉālum, nalāṭcimuṟikaḷāl peṟṟa potumakkaḷ ātaraviṉālumē avaṉ aracaṉāka muṭintatu. avaṉukku uḷnāṭṭilum veḷināṭṭilum etirikaḷ palar. vaṭakkē skāṭlāntil ṭakḷas eṉṟa paṭittalivaṉum mēṟkē vēls talivaṉ ōveṉ kiḷeṉṭōvarum avaṉukku aṭaṅkātavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷ. atōṭu avviraṇ ṭelliyilum uḷḷa iraṇṭu peruṅkuṭiyiṉarum avaṉukku muṉpōl uṭpakiyāyiṉar. avaruḷ vēls elliyilirunta mārc kōmāṉ eṭmaṉṭ mārṭṭimar riccarṭiṟkup piṉ varaṉmuṟippaṭi aracaṉāka vēṇṭiyavaṉ. iṉṉoruvaṉ skāṭlāntu elliyiluḷḷa nārtamparlantup perumakaṉ. ivaṉ heṉrikku aracaṉāka utaviyavaṉ. ivaṉ makaṉ heṉri persi eṉṟa hāṭpasparukku mārṭṭimariṉ taṅkiyi maṇampurivittataṉ mūlam ivaṉ mārṭṭimar naṭpip peṟṟāṉ. atōṭu mārṭṭimar vēls talivaṉāl ciṟip paṭuttap paṭṭapōtu avaṉ putalviyik kātalittu maṇantāṉ. skāṭlāntup paṭittalivaṉ ṭakḷasum iṅkilāntu mītu paṭiyeṭuttuka hāṭsparāl ciṟip piṭikkap paṭṭāṉ. aracaṉ avaṉit taṉ kiyil oppaṭikkumāṟu kēṭṭataṟku hāṭspar mārṭṭimari mīṭka vaṟpuṟuttiṉāṉ. aracaṉ ataṟku iṇaṅkāmiyāl ṭakḷasiṟku viṭutali tantu avaṉiyum naṇpaṉākkiṉāṉ. iṅṅaṉam ellā etirikaḷum oṉṟu cērntu kiḷarcci ceytu ṣrūspari eṉṉumiṭattil aracaṉi etirkkak kāttiruntaṉar.
hāṭsparuṭaṉ otta akaviyuṭiya heṉri iḷavaracaṉ. avaṉukku nērmāṟāyk kīḻmakkaḷ kūṭṭuṟavi virumpi avarkaḷuṭaṉ kīḻāṉa viṭutikaḷil cuṟṟi alintāṉ. avaṉuṭaṉ tōḻarā iruntavaruḷ nakiyaracaṉāṉa sarjāṉ ḵpālsṭāḵpum oruvaṉ. kāṭshil eṉṟa iṭattil avarkaḷ naṭattiya ciṟu tiruṭṭil kūṭa avaṉ uṭaṉ kalantu kāvalaruṭiyil ḵpālsṭāḵpi ēḷaṉam ceytāṉ. āṉāl, tantik ketirāṉa kiḷarcci paṟṟik kēṭṭatum avaṉ kuṇam māṟi ḵpālsṭāḵp mutaliya palarutaviyāl periya nāṭṭup paṭiyit tiraṭṭik koṇṭu ṣrūspari ceṉṟāṉ. kiḷarccikārariṭiyil kōlittaṉattāl nārtamparlantu vilaki niṟka, hāṭspariṉ muṉ ciṉattāl kiḷeṉṭōvarum vilaki niṉṟāṉ. kiḷeṉṭōvariṉ marumakaṉākiya mārṭṭimarum vēṇṭāveṟuppākac caṇṭi ceytāṉ. kiḷarccikkārar tōṟṟaṉar. hāṭspar heṉri iḷavaracaṉ kiyāl iṟantāṉ. ḵpālsṭāḵp iṟanta hāṭsparit tūkkic ceṉṟu taṉ kivēli eṉṟu kūṟik kēli nāṭakam naṭittāṉ.
eppakkam veṉṟālum ataṉuṭaṉ cērvateṉṟu kāttu niṉṟa nārtamparlantu makaṉ iṟanta ceyti kēṭṭut tikittāṉ. yārk talimakaṉ tōṟṟuvikkum iraṇṭām kiḷarcciyil cērntu paḻivāṅka vēṇṭumeṉṟa eṇṇattit tuyarāl maṉamuṟinta avaṉ maṉiviyum putalviyum taṭukka, iraṇṭām muṟiyum avaṉ kiḷarccikkārari ēmāṟavittāṉ. heṉri iḷavaracaṉ vēls talivaṉi aṭakkac ceṉṟiruntāl jāṉ iḷavaracaṉ aracar paṭittalimi tāṅkiṉāṉ. avaṉ tūtar mūlam kiḷarccip paṭiyōr kuṟikaḷi akaṟṟuvatākak kūṟi avarkaḷik kalittāṉ. iḷavaracaṉuṭaṉ tuṇit talivaṉāyirunta vesṭu mōrlantiṉ cūḻccittiṟattāl iḷavaracaṉ paṭimaṭṭum kaliyātiruntatu. kiḷarccit talivar ēmāntu ciṟippaṭṭaṉar.
mutumiyālum tuyarkaḷālum piṇiyālum nalivuṟṟu maṉṉaṉ paṭukkiyil kiṭantāṉ. avaṉ cōrvip piriveṉṟeṇṇi iḷavaracaṉ tuyarum, taṉakkup poṟuppu vantuṟṟatē eṉṟa eṇṇamum unta, muṭiyuṭaṉ kaṭavuḷi vaṇaṅkac ceṉṟiruntāṉ. ataṟkiṭiyē viḻitteḻunta maṉṉaṉ ataṟkuḷ muṭiyik kavara avaṉ virintāṉ eṉṟeṇṇi aiyamum ciṉamum koṇṭāṉ. piṉ avaṉ heṉri iḷavaracaṉ coṟkaḷāl uṇmi yuṇarntu makiḻvuṭaṉ  uyirnīttāṉ.
iḷavaracaṉ heṉri taṉ muṉṉik kuṇam maṟantu putu maṉitaṉāy iṅkilāntiṉ oppaṟṟa aracaṉāṉāṉ. naṟkuṇamuṭiyavaṉāyiṉum, aracaṉit taṉ  vaḻiyilirukka muyaṉṟa ḵpālḵṭāpḵp kūṭṭattiṉar puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭaṉar.  avarkaḷi iḷavaracaṉuṭaṉ caṭṭappaṭi taṇṭitta sarheṉrikāskāyiṉ eṉṟa taṇṭattalivaṉ naṉku matittup pōṟṟap paṭṭāṉ.
1. tuṇikkaruvikaḷē pakikkaruvikaḷāṉatu
 piṟappurimiyāl aracarākātu kuṭikaḷ iṇakkam peṟṟu aracirukki ēṟiya āṅkila maṉṉaruḷ1 nāṉkām heṉri aracaṉ oruvaṉ.
avaṉukkumuṉ āṇṭa2  iraṇṭām riccarṭu maṉṉaṉukku avaṉ ciṟṟappaṉ makaṉ. āṉāl, avaṉ tantikkum mūttavaṉāṉa iṉṉoru ciṟṟappaṉuṭiya makaṉ oruvaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉ peyar3 eṭmaṉṭu mārṭimar. aracurimi oḻuṅkuppaṭi avaṉē aracaṉāy vantirukka vēṇṭum. āṉāl, riccarṭiṉ taṉṉāṇmi āṭciyip perumakkaḷ etirttapōtu, heṉritāṉ avarkaḷukkut talimi pūṇṭāṉ. eṉavē, riccarṭu muṭi tuṟantapōtu avaṉē apperumakkaḷiṉ iṇakkattālum tuṇiyālum taṉṉi aracaṉākkik koḷḷa muṭintatu.
taṉṉalattiṉ vayappaṭṭu riccarṭiṉ pōkkik kaṇṭittē heṉri aracaṉāṉāṉ. āyiṉum aṅṅaṉam aracaṉāṉapiṉ avaṉum anta riccarṭip pōlavē, taṉṉāṇmiyuṭaṉ aracāḷat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. appōtu perumakkaḷ, riccarṭi veṟuttatativiṭa mikutiyāka avaṉi veṟukkat toṭaṅkiṉar. “riccarṭāvatu muṟippaṭi aracaṉāyiruntavaṉ; appaṭiyallaṉē ivaṉ; ivaṉ maṇimuṭi nam kippaṭak koṭutta koṭitāṉē; ivaṉukkeṉṉa ittaṉi taṉṉāṇmi?” eṉṟu avarkaḷ cīṟiṉar.
mēlum riccarṭiṉ taṉṉāṇmikku avaṉ iṉpaviruppum cōmpal vāḻvumē kāraṇam. taṉṉalamaṉṟu. aracaṉ eṉṟa muṟiyilallātu taṉimaṉitaṉ eṉṟa muṟiyil avaṉ naṟkuṇamum pīṭum perun taṉmiyum uṭiyavaṉ. heṉriyō itaṟku māṟākat taṉṉalamum cūḻcciyum uṭiyavaṉ. eṭutta kāriyatti niṟivēṟṟum vakiyil avaṉ ettakiya muṟiyiyum kiyāḷat tayaṅkātavaṉ. avaṉ kiyil aracurimi vantatē. avaṉtāṉ riccarṭiṉ koṭumiyōṭukūṭa avaṉiṭam illāta ciṟumiyum uṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu kāṭṭikkoṇṭāṉ.
heṉri, riccarṭi akaṟṟittāṉē aracaṉākap pōkum nāṭkaḷil avaṉi aracaviyil etirttuniṉṟu, “aracurimi teyvīkamākavum piṟappurimippaṭiyum varuvatu; teyvattālaṉṟi maṉitar evarālum aḵtu eṭukkappaṭavō koṭukkappaṭavō kūṭiyataṉṟu” eṉṟu 1kārlil talimakaṉ kūṟiyiruntāṉ. ataṉ payaṉāka heṉri avaṉit tūkkiliṭa nērntatu. camayattuṟiyiṉuḷ talimiyāṉa oruvaṉ. naṭunili urimi kūṟiyataṟkāka uyiriḻantatu kaṇṭu, accamayat tuṟiyiluḷḷa talimakkaḷ ellām piṟarum heṉriyiṭam pakimi koḷḷalāyiṉar.
makkaḷ heṉriyi veṟukkuntōṟum riccarṭiṉ naṟkuṇaṅkaḷiyum peruntaṉmiyiyum ōyntu avaṉi oḻittu viṭṭōmē eṉṟu kaḻivirakkamuṅ koḷḷalā yiṉar. riccarṭiṉ kālattil avaṉuk ketirākak kiḷarcci ceytavaruḷ mutaṉmiyāṉa 2nārtamparlantuk kōmakaṉtāṉ ippōtu heṉriyi etirppatilum mutalvaṉāyiruntāṉ.
ivvuṭpakivarēyaṉṟi heṉri aracaṉukku veḷippakivarum vēṟu cilar iruntaṉar.  avarē vēls nāṭṭut talivaṉāṉa 3ōveṉ kiḷeṉṭōvarum, skāṭlantup paṭittalivaṉ 4ṭakḷasum āvar. iruvaruḷ ōveṉkiḷēṉṭōvar piṟa vēls makkaḷip pōlavē vēls nāṭṭiṉ paḻiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷilum, puṉiviyal katikaḷilum teyva varalāṟṟilum paṟṟuṭiyavaṉāyiṉum, iṅkilāntiṉ palkalik kaḻakap payiṟciyuṭiyavaṉāyiṉum, iṅkilāntiṉ palkalik kaḻakap payiṟciyuṭiyavaṉātaliṉ, putu nākarika mēṟpūccum eytap peṟṟavaṉ. avaṉ talivaṉāṉa piṉ aṭikkaṭi ellippuṟattil vantu cūṟiyāṭuvāṉ. avaṉum, avaṉippōlavē skāṭlantu elliyil ṭakḷasum āṅkila aracukku iru muḷpōl tontaravu ceytu koṇṭiruntaṉar.
ivviru pakivarkaḷiṭamiruntum nāṭṭip pātukākkavē āṅkila aracar iraṇṭu elliyilum iru peruṅkuṭikaḷic celvākkuṭaṉum valimiyuṭaṉum viṭṭu vittiruntaṉar. avarkaḷē nārtamparlantup perumakaṉum mārṭimarum āvar.
āṉāl, vēliyē payiri aḻikkat toṭaṅkiyatu pōl, ivvira peruṅkuṭikaḷum avveḷippakikaḷi etirttu aracaṉi valuppaṭuttuvataṟku māṟākat tāmē uṭpakiyāyt taḻittu avveḷip pakiyuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭu aracaṉukku ōr uyirppoṟiyāy amintaṉar.
ivaruḷ ōveṉ kiḷeṉṭōvar orukāl pōril ellip puṟak kuṭiyiṉaṉākiya eṭmaṉṭu mārṭimarip piṭittuc ciṟiyil vittiruntāṉ. ciṟiyiliruntē mārṭimar kiḷeṉṭōvariṉ aḻakiṟ ciṟanta putalviyuṭaṉ naṭpuk koṇṭu avaḷi maṇantu koṇṭāṉ. itaṉāl   mārṭimarukkum kiḷeṉṭōvarukkumiṭiyē irunta paḻam pakimi nīṅki avarkaḷiṭayē iṇiyaṟā uṟavu ēṟpaṭṭatu.
mārṭimariṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāḷ. ēṟkeṉavē nārtampar lantiṉ makaṉākiya hāṭspar eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya 1heṉri persiyi maṇantiruntāḷ. eṉavē, kiḷeṉṭōvaruṭaṉ mārṭimarum nārtamparlantum āṅkila aracarkketirāy oṉṟu cērntuviṭṭaṉar.
inniliyil aracaṉ nārtamparlantiṉ vaḷarcci paṟṟi iṉṉum accamum aḻukkāṟum koḷḷac ceyyum ceyal oṉṟu nikaḻntatu.
skāṭlāntu vīraṉāṉa ṭakḷas iṅkilāntiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttu nārtampalantāl muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ. ituvari eppōrilum puṟaṅkoṭā vīraṉākiya avaṉ mikunta ūkkattuṭaṉ irupattiyāyiram pērkoṇṭa perumpaṭi oṉṟu tiraṭṭi mīṇṭum paṭiyeṭuttāṉ. ittaṭavi hāṭspar eṉṟa iḷaṅkāḷi avaṉiyum avaṉ periya paṭiyiyum muṉṉiyilum paṉmaṭaṅku naliya muṭiyaṭittu avaṉiyum avaṉ vīrarkaḷ palariyum ciṟip piṭittāṉ. atōṭu pōrk karuvikaḷum poruṭkuviyum aḷavilātu avaṉ kiyuṭpaṭṭaṉa.
veḷippakiyākiya ṭakḷasi viṭa uṭpakiyākiya taṉ peruṅ kuṭiyiṉukkē mikutiyāka añciya heṉri, taṉ cūḻcciyāl akkuṭiyiṉari eppaṭiyāvatu taṉ vayappaṭutta eṇṇi, avarkaḷi aḻittu nayavañcaka moḻiyāl avarkaḷ vīrattiyum aracup paṟṟiyum pukaḻntu, piṉ naḷiṉamākap pōril piṭipaṭṭa ciṟiyāḷikaḷiyum poruṭkuviyiṉiyum tarumāṟu kēṭṭāṉ. nārtamparlantu avaṟṟi viṭṭukkoṭukka maṉamillātapaṭi takkāṭṭiṉāṉ. arukilirunta vorsṭar mutaliya peruṅkuṭi makkaḷum avaṉiyē peritum tāṅki niṉṟaṉar.
āṉāl, nārtamparlantiṉ makaṉāṉa heṉri hāṭspar taṉ tanti mutaliyavaruṭiya veḷippūccu muṟikaḷi virumpātu nēriṭiyākavē aracaṉi etirkka eṇṇiṉāṉ. eṇṇi avaṉ. ōveṉa kiḷeṉṭōvariṭam atupōtu ciṟipukuntuḷḷa taṉ mittuṉaṉākiya eṭmaṉṭu mārṭimaric ciṟimīṭṭuk koṭuppatāyiṉ, tāṉ ṭakḷasiyum piṟa ciṟiyāḷikaḷiyum koṭuppatākak kūṟiṉāṉ.
ataṟku heṉri, “uṇmiyil etiri pakkamē cērntuviṭṭa nāṭṭup pakivaṉāṉa mārṭimari nāṭṭup paṇattilirunta mīṭpatu eṉpatu veṭkakkēṭu” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. hāṭspar ciṉantu, “eṉa mittuṉaṉiyā nāṭṭup pakivaṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvatu? nāṭṭup pakivaṉtāṉ nāṭṭukkākap pōrpurintu paṭukāyam aṭivaṉō!” eṉṟu vīṟuṭaṉ pēci akaṉṟāṉ.
taṉṉi aracaṉākka mikutiyum utaviya antak kuṭiyē iṉṟu taṉakku nēr pakiyākip piṟa pakivarkaḷiyum tūṇṭi oṉṟucērttu varuvatu kaṇṭu heṉri mikavum maṉam puḻuṅkiṉāṉ.
